By coupling global curved-fractal geometry with local spiral-based appearance interference, CFGPatch disrupts both shape perception and texture interpretation. We further adopt expectation over transformation (EOT) to improve robustness against common image-level transformations. Extensive experiments show that CFGPatch effectively fools VIS-IR VLMs and consistently outperforms standard patch baselines in attack effectiveness and robustness. Moreover, adversarial samples optimized for zero-shot classification transfer well to image captioning and visual question answering, demonstrating strong cross-task transferability and generalizability across downstream tasks.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉语言模型（VLMs）在多种多模态任务中展现出强大的性能，但它们在可见光-红外（VIS-IR）场景下的对抗鲁棒性仍未得到充分探索。这一空白至关重要，因为VIS-IR感知被广泛应用于现实世界的感知系统中，以支持在具有挑战性的成像条件下实现可靠的感知理解。为了解决这一跨模态威胁场景，我们提出了CFGPatch——一种用于攻击VIS-IR VLMs的弯曲边缘分形几何对抗补丁框架。CFGPatch基于三角形分形几何，用贝塞尔曲线元素替代刚性直线基元，在保持多尺度分形自相似性的同时引入了更平滑的轮廓、更丰富的方向变化以及更灵活的形变能力。此外，我们设计了一种模态特定的Fraser-spiral渲染机制，向可见光和红外图像注入细粒度的纹理失真和误导性感知线索。通过将全局弯曲分形几何与局部螺旋式外观干扰相结合，CFGPatch同时破坏了形状感知和纹理解释。我们进一步采用期望变换（EOT）来增强对常见图像级变换的鲁棒性。大量实验表明，CFGPatch能够有效欺骗VIS-IR VLMs，并且在攻击有效性和鲁棒性上持续优于标准补丁基线方法。此外，针对零样本分类优化的对抗样本能够很好地迁移到图像描述和视觉问答任务中，展现出跨下游任务的强迁移性和泛化能力。

</details>


### [60] [PIU: Proximity-guided Identity Unlearning in ID-Conditioned Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22311)
*Jose Edgar Hernandez Cancino Estrada,Mauro Díaz Lupone,Žiga Emeršič,Vitomir Štruc,Peter Peer,Darian Tomašević*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向身份条件扩散模型的遗忘学习框架PIU，通过将目标身份映射到锚定身份并微调少量交叉注意力层，有效实现人脸生成中的身份遗忘，同时保持其他身份的一致性与生成质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 身份条件扩散模型虽然能生成高质量且身份一致的人脸，但也带来了严重的隐私问题——模型可能在被遗忘后继续合成特定个体的图像。现有机器遗忘研究多关注概念或数据移除，针对身份遗忘的研究尚属空白，尤其是在直接基于身份嵌入而非文本提示进行条件控制的模型中。

Method: 提出邻近引导的身份遗忘（PIU）框架：1）将身份移除构建为身份替换目标，在学到的身份空间中将源身份重新分配给选定的锚定身份；2）基于ArcFace表示几何特性设计邻近性锚定选择策略；3）仅对少量身份敏感的交叉注意力层进行局部微调即可实现有效遗忘。

Result: 在多个目标身份上的实验表明，该框架有效抑制了目标身份的生成，同时保持保留身份的真实性与一致性，在遗忘指标和图像质量指标上均有提升，并通过定性评估得到验证。

Conclusion: 本文提出的PIU框架能够通过锚定引导和局部微调的方式，在Arc2Face这类身份条件扩散模型中实现高效的身份遗忘，为隐私保护的人脸生成提供了可行方案。

Original Abstract: Identity-conditioned diffusion models enable high-quality and identity-consistent face generation, but they also raise severe privacy concerns, as models may continue to synthesize individuals despite their right to be forgotten. While machine unlearning has been extensively studied for concept and data removal, identity unlearning remains largely unexplored, particularly in models conditioned directly on identity embeddings rather than text prompts. In this work, we study identity unlearning in Arc2Face, a state-of-the-art identity-conditioned latent diffusion model for face generation, and introduce Proximity-guided Identity Unlearning (PIU), an anchor-guided framework for identity unlearning. Specifically, we formulate identity removal as an identity replacement objective that reassigns the source identity to a selected anchor identity in the learned identity space, and we complement it with a proximity-based anchor selection strategy motivated by the geometry of ArcFace representations. We further show that effective unlearning can be achieved through localized fine-tuning of a small subset of identity-sensitive cross-attention layers. Experiments across many target identities show that our framework effectively suppresses generation of the target identity while preserving realism and identity consistency for retained identities, as validated by improved performance on unlearning and image-quality metrics, together with qualitative evaluation. The source code for the PIU framework is publicly available at https://github.com/edgarcancinoe/piu_unlearning .

中文摘要翻译: 身份条件扩散模型能够实现高质量且身份一致的人脸生成，但也引发了严重的隐私问题——即便个体行使被遗忘权后，模型仍可能继续合成其面部图像。虽然机器遗忘在概念移除和数据移除方面已被广泛研究，但身份遗忘仍是一个尚未充分探索的领域，尤其是在直接基于身份嵌入而非文本提示进行条件控制的模型中。本文以Arc2Face——一种最先进的身份条件潜在扩散人脸生成模型——为研究对象，提出邻近引导的身份遗忘（PIU），一种基于锚定引导的身份遗忘框架。具体而言，我们将身份移除构建为一个身份替换目标，在学到的身份空间中将源身份重新分配给一个选定的锚定身份，并辅以基于ArcFace表示几何特性设计的邻近性锚定选择策略。我们进一步证明，通过对少量身份敏感的交叉注意力层进行局部微调即可实现有效的遗忘。跨多个目标身份的实验表明，我们的框架在有效抑制目标身份生成的同时，保留了剩余身份的真实性与一致性，这在遗忘指标和图像质量指标的改进以及定性评估中均得到了验证。PIU框架的源代码已在https://github.com/edgarcancinoe/piu_unlearning 公开发布。

</details>


### [61] [Robustness of breast lesion segmentation under MRI undersampling improves with k-space-aware deep learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22327)
*Lukas T. Rotkopf,Marco Schlimbach,Julius C. Holzschuh,Heinz-Peter Schlemmer,Jens Kleesiek,Moritz Rempe*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究探究从MRI原始k空间直接学习乳腺病变分割的可行性，发现k空间感知的混合模型在欠采样和噪声条件下比传统图像域模型更鲁棒，同时在全采样下性能相当。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 评估能否直接从采集的MRI k空间学习乳腺病变分割，以及这样做是否能在数据加速或含噪时提升模型鲁棒性。

Method: 使用公开乳腺DCE-MRI数据集，比较四种3D U-Net变体：混合k空间-图像模型、原生k空间模型、幅度图像基线、复数图像基线。在递增欠采样和添加复高斯k空间噪声条件下评估，以Dice相似系数为指标进行交叉验证。

Result: 全采样时混合模型与图像域模型性能相似；随加速增加，混合模型显著保留更多分割精度，在中高欠采样水平显著优于幅度图像基线。加噪声时混合模型退化更慢，图像基线在重噪声下失效。特征分析表明k空间阶段和图像阶段起互补作用，频域滤波集中在图像域病灶定位之前。

Conclusion: k空间感知的深度学习在MRI欠采样和k空间噪声条件下提升了乳腺病变分割的鲁棒性，同时在全采样时保持与图像域方法相当的性能。

Original Abstract: Purpose: To assess whether breast lesion segmentation can be learned directly from acquired MRI k-space, and whether doing so improves robustness when data are accelerated or noisy.
  Materials and Methods: This retrospective study used public breast dynamic contrast-enhanced MRI (DCE-MRI) datasets with acquired and synthetic k-space, together with a within-dataset synthetic control. We compared four 3D U-Net variants: a hybrid k-space-to-image model, a native k-space model, and magnitude and complex image-space baselines. Models were evaluated under increasing undersampling and added complex Gaussian k-space noise. The primary outcome was patient-level Dice similarity coefficient under cross-validation, with the hybrid model prespecified as the main comparison against the magnitude image-space baseline.
  Results: At full sampling, the hybrid and image-space models performed similarly. As acceleration increased, the hybrid model retained substantially more segmentation accuracy and significantly outperformed the magnitude image-space baseline across moderate to high undersampling levels. The same pattern was observed when noise was added directly to k-space: the hybrid model degraded more slowly, whereas the image-space baseline failed under heavier noise. This advantage was reproduced in the within-dataset synthetic control. Feature analysis suggested that the k-space stage and image-space stage played complementary roles, with frequency-domain filtering concentrated before image-domain lesion localization.
  Conclusion: K-space-aware deep learning improves the robustness of breast lesion segmentation under MRI undersampling and k-space noise, while matching image-space methods at full sampling.

中文摘要翻译: 目的：评估能否直接从采集的MRI k空间中学习乳腺病变分割，以及这样做是否能在数据加速或含噪声时提升鲁棒性。材料与方法：本回顾性研究使用公开的乳腺动态对比增强MRI（DCE-MRI）数据集，包含采集和合成的k空间数据，以及一个数据集内的合成对照。我们比较了四种3D U-Net变体：混合k空间-图像模型、原生k空间模型、幅度图像基线和复数图像基线。模型在递增欠采样和添加复高斯k空间噪声条件下进行评估。主要结局指标为交叉验证下的患者水平Dice相似系数，混合模型被预先指定为与幅度图像基线的主要比较对象。结果：在全采样条件下，混合模型和图像域模型性能相似。随着加速程度增加，混合模型保留了显著更多的分割精度，在中高欠采样水平下显著优于幅度图像基线。当噪声直接添加到k空间时也观察到相同模式：混合模型退化更慢，而图像基线在较重噪声下失效。这一优势在数据集内的合成对照中得到了复现。特征分析表明，k空间阶段和图像阶段发挥了互补作用，频域滤波集中在图像域病灶定位之前。结论：k空间感知的深度学习在MRI欠采样和k空间噪声条件下提升了乳腺病变分割的鲁棒性，同时在全采样时匹配图像域方法的性能。

</details>


### [62] [3D LULC classification using multispectral LiDAR and deep learning: current and prospective schemes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22328)
*Narges Takhtkeshha,Aldino Rizaldy,Markus Hollaus,Juha Hyyppä,Fabio Remondino,Gottfried Mandlburger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对多光谱LiDAR点云语义分割缺乏符合国家测绘机构分类标准的数据集问题，提出了L1和L2两个级别的NMCA对齐LULC分类方案，构建了Loosdorf-MSL基准数据集，并评估了七种深度学习模型。实验表明，Point Transformer V3在双波长LiDAR系统上表现最佳（L1 mIoU 79.4%，L2 mIoU 58.9%），多光谱信息相比纯几何特征带来显著提升（L1提升1.1个百分点，L2提升7.8个百分点）。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前缺乏面向城市与郊区的、符合国家测绘与地籍机构（NMCA）分类标准的多光谱LiDAR公开数据集，限制了深度学习在该领域的应用。因此，本文旨在填补这一空白，推动多光谱LiDAR用于国家LULC分类。

Method: 提出了L1（8类）和L2（20类）两级NMCA对齐的LULC分类方案，构建了Loosdorf-MSL多光谱LiDAR基准数据集；使用双波长LiDAR系统（532 nm和1064 nm），评估了七种最先进的深度学习模型，并进行了光谱消融实验。

Result: Point Transformer V3表现最佳，L1 mIoU为79.4%，L2 mIoU为58.9%。消融实验表明，多光谱信息相比纯几何输入在L1上提升1.1个百分点，在L2上提升7.8个百分点，表明多光谱信息对细粒度材料区分具有重要价值。

Conclusion: 多光谱LiDAR反射率信息能够显著提升地物材料精细判别能力，支持NMCA的LULC分类体系向更高语义细节演进；Loosdorf-MSL数据集为国内外一致的LULC制图提供了新基准。

Original Abstract: Land Use Land Cover (LULC) classification is essential for national 3D mapping, geospatial analysis, and sustainable planning. Multispectral (MS) LiDAR provides synchronized spatial-spectral information, and deep learning (DL) enables 3D point cloud semantic segmentation; however, adoption is limited by the lack of publicly available urban and suburban MS LiDAR datasets aligned with National Mapping and Cadastral Agencies (NMCAs) classification schemes. This study addresses these gaps by introducing L1 and L2 NMCA-aligned LULC classification schemes and a new benchmark MS LiDAR dataset. We evaluate seven state-of-the-art DL models and perform spectral ablation studies at both levels of detail. Results show that Point Transformer V3 achieves the best performance, with mIoU of 79.4% (L1, 8 classes) and 58.9% (L2, 20 classes) using a dual-wavelength LiDAR system (532 nm and 1064 nm). Ablation results show that multispectral information improves performance over geometry-only inputs, with gains of 1.1 percentage points at L1 and 7.8 points at L2. These results highlight the value of LiDAR reflectance for fine-grained material discrimination and support the evolution of NMCA LULC schemes toward higher semantic detail. The Loosdorf-MSL dataset contributes a new benchmark for consistent national and international LULC mapping.

中文摘要翻译: 土地覆盖利用（LULC）分类对于国家三维制图、地理空间分析和可持续规划至关重要。多光谱激光雷达（MS LiDAR）能够提供同步的空间-光谱信息，深度学习（DL）则支持三维点云语义分割；然而，由于缺少符合国家测绘与地籍机构（NMCAs）分类体系的公开城市与郊区多光谱LiDAR数据集，其应用受到限制。本研究通过引入与NMCA对齐的L1和L2两级LULC分类方案以及一个新的基准多光谱LiDAR数据集来填补这些空白。我们评估了七种最先进的深度学习模型，并在两个细节级别上进行了光谱消融研究。结果表明，采用双波长LiDAR系统（532 nm和1064 nm）时，Point Transformer V3表现最佳，在L1（8类）和L2（20类）上的平均交并比（mIoU）分别达到79.4%和58.9%。消融结果显示，多光谱信息相比纯几何输入在性能上有所提升，在L1和L2上分别提高了1.1和7.8个百分点。这些结果突显了LiDAR反射率对于精细材料区分的价值，并支持NMCA的LULC分类体系向更高语义细节发展。Loosdorf-MSL数据集为一致的国家和国际LULC制图提供了新的基准。

</details>


### [63] [4D-GSW: Kinematic-Aware Spatio-Temporal Consistent Watermarking for 4D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22342)
*Sifan Zhou,Hang Zhang,Yuhang Wang,Ming Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出4D-GSW，一种运动感知水印框架，通过时空曲率度量、HMM-MRF能量最小化和各向异性梯度路由，在不破坏4D高斯溅射动态重建时空一致性的前提下实现鲁棒版权保护。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有隐写方法忽视了4D动态场景的底层运动流形，导致非物理想象（如时间闪烁和FVD坍塌），需要一种能保持高时空一致性的运动感知水印方案。

Method: 1) 提出时空曲率(STC)度量识别"动态瞬间"，自适应门控水印梯度注入以保护关键运动流形；2) 构建HMM-MRF联合能量最小化模型，在时间轨迹和空间邻域同步水印相位；3) 设计各向异性梯度路由机制，确保水印嵌入与光度重建保真度严格解耦。

Result: 实验表明该方法在鲁棒隐藏水印、抵抗多种攻击的同时，保持了高渲染质量和时空一致性。

Conclusion: 4D-GSW有效解决了4D动态场景水印中的时空一致性问题，实现了版权保护与高质量动态渲染的兼顾。

Original Abstract: While 4D Gaussian Splatting (4DGS) has revolutionized high-fidelity dynamic reconstruction, safeguarding the intellectual property of these assets remains an open challenge. Conventional steganographic techniques often neglect the underlying kinematic manifolds, triggering non-physical artifacts such as severe temporal flickering and "FVD collapse". To address this, we propose \textbf{4D-GSW}, a kinematic-aware watermarking framework designed to embed robust copyright information while preserving high spatio-temporal consistency. Unlike prior 4D steganography that primarily focuses on opacity-guided invisibility, our approach explicitly addresses the physical coherence of motion trajectories. We introduce a \textbf{Spatio-Temporal Curvature (STC)} metric to identify "Dynamic Instants," adaptively gating watermark gradient injection to shield critical motion manifolds from non-physical perturbations. To ensure global coherence across complex deformations, we formulate a joint \textbf{HMM-MRF energy minimization} model that synchronizes watermark phases within both temporal trajectories and spatial neighborhoods. Furthermore, an \textbf{anisotropic gradient routing} mechanism ensures that watermark embedding remains strictly decoupled from photometric reconstruction fidelity. Extensive experiments have demonstrated the superior performance of our method in robustly hiding watermarks while resisting various attacks and maintaining high rendering quality and spatiotemporal consistency.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管4D高斯溅射(4DGS)革新了高保真动态重建，但保护这些资产的知识产权仍是一个开放挑战。传统的隐写技术往往忽略底层运动流形，引发非物理想象，如严重的时间闪烁和"FVD坍塌"。为解决这一问题，我们提出\textbf{4D-GSW}，一种运动感知水印框架，旨在嵌入鲁棒版权信息的同时保持高时空一致性。不同于以往主要关注透明度引导不可见性的4D隐写方法，我们的方法明确处理运动轨迹的物理连续性。我们引入\textbf{时空曲率(STC)}度量来识别"动态瞬间"，自适应门控水印梯度注入，以保护关键运动流形免受非物理扰动。为确保复杂形变下的全局一致性，我们构建了一个联合\textbf{HMM-MRF能量最小化}模型，在时间轨迹和空间邻域内同步水印相位。此外，\textbf{各向异性梯度路由}机制确保水印嵌入与光度重建保真度严格解耦。大量实验表明，我们的方法在鲁棒隐藏水印、抵抗各种攻击以及保持高渲染质量和时空一致性方面均表现出优越性能。

</details>


### [64] [Bernini: Latent Semantic Planning for Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22344)
*Bernini Team,Chenchen Liu,Junyi Chen,Lei Li,Lu Chi,Mingzhen Sun,Zhuoying Li,Yi Fu,Ruoyu Guo,Yiheng Wu,Ge Bai,Zehuan Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Bernini统一框架，将MLLM（语义规划）与扩散模型（像素渲染）分工协作，实现视频生成与编辑的SOTA性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: MLLM擅长多模态语义推理，扩散模型擅长逼真像素渲染，两者各有所长但未有效统一；作者认为可通过简单的分工协作将它们结合起来，发挥各自优势。

Method: (1) MLLM规划器将目标语义表示直接预测到ViT嵌入空间；(2) DiT渲染器基于该规划、文本特征及源VAE特征（编辑任务）合成像素；(3) 语义作为接口，规划器与渲染器可分离训练、轻量联合微调；(4) 提出SA-3D RoPE处理多视觉输入；(5) 在规划器中引入思维链推理增强理解到生成的迁移。

Result: 在多个视频生成与编辑基准上达到SOTA性能，MLLM的预训练理解能力在复杂编辑任务中展现出强泛化性。

Conclusion: 提出MLLM与扩散模型统一框架Bernini，通过语义规划与像素渲染的分工协作，兼顾了两类模型的优势，实现了高效训练与优异性能。

Original Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) and diffusion models have each reached remarkable maturity: MLLMs excel at reasoning over heterogeneous multimodal inputs with strong semantic grounding, while diffusion models synthesize images and videos with photorealistic fidelity. We argue that these two families can be unified through a simple division of labor: MLLMs perform semantic planning, while diffusion models render pixels from high-level semantic guidance and low-level visual features. Building on this idea, we propose Bernini, a unified framework for video generation and editing. An MLLM-based planner predicts the target semantic representation directly in the ViT embedding space, and a DiT-based renderer synthesizes pixels conditioned on this plan, augmented by text features and, for editing, source VAE features for detail preservation. Because semantics serve as the interface, the planner and renderer can be trained separately and only lightly co-trained, preserving the pretrained strengths of both components while keeping training efficient. To better handle multiple visual inputs, we introduce Segment-Aware 3D Rotary Positional Embedding (SA-3D RoPE), and further incorporate chain-of-thought reasoning in the planner to better transfer understanding into generation. Bernini achieves state-of-the-art performance across a wide range of video generation and editing benchmarks, with the MLLM's pretrained understanding translating into strong generalization on challenging editing tasks.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态大语言模型（MLLM）和扩散模型各自已相当成熟：MLLM擅长对异构多模态输入进行推理，具有强大的语义基础；而扩散模型能够以照片级逼真度合成图像和视频。我们认为，这两类模型可以通过简单的分工实现统一：MLLM负责语义规划，扩散模型根据高层语义引导和低层视觉特征渲染像素。基于此思想，我们提出了Bernini，一个统一的视频生成与编辑框架。基于MLLM的规划器直接在ViT嵌入空间中预测目标语义表示，基于DiT的渲染器则根据该规划及文本特征（编辑任务中还包括源VAE特征以保留细节）合成像素。由于语义作为接口，规划器和渲染器可以分开训练，仅需轻量级联合微调，在保持两方面预训练优势的同时提高训练效率。为了更好地处理多视觉输入，我们引入了分段感知的3D旋转位置编码（SA-3D RoPE），并在规划器中融入思维链推理，以更好地将理解迁移到生成中。Bernini在广泛的视频生成与编辑基准上达到了最先进的性能，MLLM的预训练理解能力在具有挑战性的编辑任务中展现出强大的泛化能力。

</details>


### [65] [VEELA: A Clinically-Constrained Benchmark for Liver Vessel Segmentation in Computed Tomography Angiography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22357)
*Ziya Ata Yazıcı,N. Sinem Gezer,İlkay Öksüz,İlker Özgür Koska,Tuğçe Toprak,Pervin Bulucu,Ufuk Beşenk,A. Emre Kavur,Pierre-Henri Conze,Hazım Kemal Ekenel,Oğuz Dicle,Mustafa Ege Şeker,Mustafa Said Kartal,Ariorad Moniri,Orhan Özkan,Osman Faruk Bayram,Hakan Polat,Musa Balcı,Ece Tuğba Cebeci,Baran Cılga,Kardelen Peçenek,M. Alper Selver*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了VEELA数据集，一个基于40例CTA扫描、经过严格专家共识手工标注的肝脏血管数据集，并建立了标准化评估框架，揭示了不同评估指标（如clDice、IoU、NSD、面积、长度）对血管分割评估的差异，强调了多视角评估的必要性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有公开的肝脏血管分割数据集缺乏临床真实的标注约束（如基于可见性而非解剖推断的标注策略），难以捕捉血管拓扑复杂性和成像不确定性，导致分割方法评估不够可靠。

Method: 从CHAOS挑战的40例CTA扫描出发，采用多专家逐层手工标注、基于可见性驱动的标注策略，避免解剖推断插值；并建立包含拓扑感知（clDice）、重叠度（IoU）、边界敏感（NSD）、几何度量（面积、长度）的综合评估框架。

Result: 不同评估指标（clDice、IoU、NSD、面积、长度）捕捉了血管完整性的不同方面，指标间存在明显差异，表明单指标评估不足以全面反映临床意义的血管分割质量。

Conclusion: VEELA数据集和评估框架为肝脏血管分割提供了更真实的基准，多视角评估对于临床有意义的血管分割至关重要，数据集已公开以促进可重复研究。

Original Abstract: Accurate segmentation of hepatic and portal vessels in contrast-enhanced computed tomography angiography (CTA) remains challenging due to complex vascular topology, peripheral visibility limitations, and acquisition-induced ambiguities. While existing public datasets offer valuable benchmarks, few include clinically realistic annotation constraints. We introduce VEELA (Vessel Extraction and Extrication for Liver Analysis), a rigorously curated liver vessel dataset derived from 40 CTA scans inherited from the CHAOS grand-challenge cohort. All vessels were manually delineated slice-by-slice under multi-expert consensus, using a strict visibility-driven annotation policy and avoiding anatomically inferred interpolation. This design explicitly captures anatomical variability and imaging-related uncertainty. As a continuation of the CHAOS challenge, VEELA enables reproducible cross-benchmark evaluation while extending the scope to fine-grained hepatic and portal vessel segmentation. We further establish a standardized benchmarking framework and analyze complementary evaluation metrics, including topology-aware (clDice), overlap-based (IoU), boundary-sensitive (NSD), and geometry-aware (area, length) measures. Our results demonstrate that different metrics capture distinct aspects of vascular integrity, underscoring the necessity of multi-perspective evaluation for clinically meaningful vessel segmentation. VEELA is publicly released to facilitate reproducible research and support the development of robust vascular segmentation methods. Researchers can access the evaluation metrics, dataset, and submission platform at https://www.synapse.org/Synapse:syn65471967.

中文摘要翻译: 在对比增强CT血管造影（CTA）中，由于复杂的血管拓扑结构、外周可见性限制以及采集过程引入的模糊性，肝静脉和门静脉的准确分割仍然具有挑战性。尽管现有的公开数据集提供了有价值的基准，但很少包含临床现实的标注约束。我们提出了VEELA（Vessel Extraction and Extrication for Liver Analysis），这是一个从CHAOS大挑战队列中继承的40例CTA扫描中严格筛选的肝脏血管数据集。所有血管均在多专家共识下逐层手工勾画，采用严格的可见性驱动标注策略，避免基于解剖推断的插值。这种设计明确捕捉了解剖变异性和成像相关的不确定性。作为CHAOS挑战的延续，VEELA在将范围扩展到细粒度肝静脉和门静脉分割的同时，实现了可重复的跨基准评估。我们进一步建立了一个标准化的基准测试框架，分析了互补的评估指标，包括拓扑感知度量（clDice）、重叠度量（IoU）、边界敏感度量（NSD）以及几何感知度量（面积、长度）。我们的结果表明，不同指标捕捉了血管完整性的不同方面，强调了多视角评估对临床有意义的血管分割的必要性。VEELA已公开发布，以促进可重复研究并支持鲁棒血管分割方法的发展。研究人员可通过https://www.synapse.org/Synapse:syn65471967访问评估指标、数据集和提交平台。

</details>


### [66] [GazePrior: Zero-Shot AR/VR Eye Tracking via Learned 3D Gaze Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22359)
*Corentin Dumery,David Colmenares,Alexander Fix,Pascal Fua,Ali Behrooz,Jogendra Kundu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种数据驱动的3D先验模型GazePrior，利用已有眼动追踪设备的标注数据进行稀疏输入3D重建，再渲染到新设备视角合成高质量训练数据，无需额外真实数据采集即可训练新设备的眼动追踪模型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 训练新AR/VR设备的眼动追踪模型面临挑战：真实数据采集成本高且耗时，而现有合成数据生成方法缺乏真实感，需要一种无需额外数据采集同时保持数据质量的方法。

Method: 提出GazePrior模型，这是一个数据驱动的3D先验，建模了跨不同身份、注视方向和光照条件下人眼分布。利用该先验，对已有眼动追踪设备采集的标注数据进行稀疏输入3D重建，然后从目标设备的相机视角渲染出合成数据，实现真实感、多样性和标注精度。

Result: 实验表明，使用该方法合成数据训练的眼动追踪模型在准确性和鲁棒性上优于以往的零样本方法。

Conclusion: 该方法无需昂贵真实数据采集即可合成高质量训练数据，为跨设备眼动追踪提供了一种高效、实用的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Eye tracking (ET) is a foundational technology for advanced AR/VR applications. However, training ET models for every new ET device is challenging: real data collection is costly and time-consuming, while existing synthetic data generation methods lack realism. To remove the need for additional data collection while maintaining data quality, we introduce a data-driven 3D prior that models the distribution of human eyes across diverse identities, gaze directions, and light settings. This model, which we coin GazePrior, then enables sparse-input 3D reconstruction of annotated data collected with previous ET devices, which can in turn be rendered from the cameras of any target ET device. Our approach synthesizes data with the realism, diversity and ground-truth accuracy of real data collection without its prohibitive costs. Our experiments demonstrate that ET models trained with our synthesized data outperform previous zero-shot methods, achieving higher accuracy and robustness.

中文摘要翻译: 眼动追踪（ET）是高级AR/VR应用的基础技术。然而，为每一个新的ET设备训练眼动追踪模型具有挑战性：真实数据采集成本高且耗时，而现有的合成数据生成方法缺乏真实感。为了在保持数据质量的同时消除额外数据采集的需求，我们引入了一个数据驱动的3D先验，该先验建模了跨不同身份、注视方向和光照条件下人眼的分布。这个我们称之为GazePrior的模型，能够对先前ET设备采集的标注数据进行稀疏输入3D重建，进而可以从任何目标ET设备的相机视角渲染出数据。我们的方法以真实数据采集的真实感、多样性和标注精度合成了数据，且无需其高昂成本。实验表明，使用我们合成数据训练的眼动追踪模型在准确性和鲁棒性上优于以往的零样本方法。

</details>


### [67] [AgroTools: A Benchmark for Tool-Augmented Multimodal Agents in Agriculture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22366)
*Zi Ye,Yibin Wen,Xiaoya Fan,Xinyu Zhang,Jing Wu,Kun Zeng,Zurong Mai,Jiarui Zhang,Bohan Shi,Juepeng Zheng,Jianxi Huang,Yutong Lu,Haohuan Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了 AgroTools——一个用于评估农业领域中工具增强型多模态智能体的基准。该基准包含539个问答实例、1097张异构农业图像、14种农业工具的执行环境，并支持过程级执行质量和结果级任务成功的双视角评估。实验表明，当前主流多模态大语言模型在该基准上表现远未达到可靠水平，在工具规划、参数生成、执行恢复和最终答案合成等方面存在明显瓶颈。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的农业多模态基准主要评估最终答案的正确性，缺乏对模型能否使用外部工具完成精度敏感工作流的评估能力。

Method: 构建了包含539个问答实例、1097张异构农业图像、5个任务族和14种可执行农业工具的基准，每个查询都标注了结构化的工具使用轨迹，支持过程级执行质量和结果级任务成功的双视角评估。

Result: 在9个开源和4个闭源多模态大语言模型上的基准测试表明，当前模型在农业工具使用场景下远未达到可靠水平，在工具规划、参数生成、执行恢复和最终答案合成方面存在明显瓶颈。

Conclusion: AgroTools基准能够支持未来面向高精度农业应用的多模态智能体研究，现有模型在该任务上仍有较大提升空间。

Original Abstract: Agricultural decision-making increasingly requires multimodal systems that can transform visual observations into reliable, executable actions. However, existing agricultural multimodal benchmarks mainly evaluate final-answer correctness and provide limited support for assessing whether models can use external tools to complete precision-sensitive workflows. In this paper, we introduce AgroTools, a benchmark for evaluating tool-augmented multimodal agents in agriculture. AgroTools contains 539 question-answer instances paired with 1,097 heterogeneous agricultural images, spanning five task families and an executable environment of 14 agricultural tools. Each query is annotated with structured tool-use traces, enabling a dual-view evaluation of both process-level execution quality and outcome-level task success. We benchmark 9 open-source and 4 closed-source multimodal large language models on AgroTools. Results show that current models remain far from reliable in agricultural tool-use settings, with clear bottlenecks in tool planning, argument generation, execution recovery, and final-answer synthesis. We hope AgroTools will support future research on multimodal agents for high-precision agricultural applications. The benchmark and evaluation are available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/AgroTools/AgroTools.

中文摘要翻译: 农业决策日益需要多模态系统将视觉观察转化为可靠、可执行的动作。然而，现有的农业多模态基准主要评估最终答案的正确性，对模型能否使用外部工具完成精度敏感工作流的支持有限。本文提出了AgroTools——一个用于评估农业中工具增强型多模态智能体的基准。AgroTools包含539个问答实例，配以1097张异构农业图像，涵盖五个任务族和包含14种农业工具的可执行环境。每个查询都标注了结构化的工具使用轨迹，支持过程级执行质量和结果级任务成功的双视角评估。我们在AgroTools上对9个开源和4个闭源多模态大语言模型进行了基准测试。结果表明，当前模型在农业工具使用场景下远未达到可靠水平，在工具规划、参数生成、执行恢复和最终答案合成方面存在明显瓶颈。我们希望AgroTools能够支持未来面向高精度农业应用的多模态智能体研究。该基准和评估方法可在https://huggingface.co/datasets/AgroTools/AgroTools获取。

</details>


### [68] [Translating Signals to Languages for sEMG-Based Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22403)
*Ming Wang,Haoxuan Qu,Qiuhong Ke,Wei Zhou,Hossein Rahmani,Jun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LLM-sEMG框架，通过将连续sEMG信号转换为“sEMG语言”，利用大语言模型的推理能力进行高精度活动识别。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有sEMG活动识别方法要么依赖更大的模型架构提升表征能力，要么通过大规模预训练增强先验知识。而大语言模型在NLP中展现出的泛化与推理能力，以及其从大量动作语言描述中学到的隐含知识，为解读sEMG信号和推断活动意图提供了新可能。

Method: 提出LLM-sEMG框架，核心包括一个语言导向的映射机制，将连续sEMG序列转换为“sEMG语言”，并集成多种策略以促进信号到语言的映射过程，从而让大语言模型直接作为sEMG活动识别器。

Result: 大量实验表明，所提框架利用大语言模型实现了高精度的sEMG信号活动识别。

Conclusion: 大语言模型能够有效作为sEMG活动识别器，通过信号到语言的映射机制可实现对sEMG信号的准确识别。

Original Abstract: Surface electromyography (sEMG) signal-based activity recognition has attracted increasing research attention in recent years. To develop accurate sEMG signal-based activity recognizers, numerous approaches have been proposed. Some studies focus on designing larger and more expressive model architectures to enhance the representational capacity of sEMG signals, while others aim to enrich model priors through large-scale pretraining, thereby improving recognition performance. Recently, large language models (LLMs) have shown remarkable generalization and reasoning capabilities in natural language processing, whose implicit knowledge, learned from extensive linguistic descriptions of actions, opens new possibilities for interpreting sEMG signals and inferring activity intentions. Motivated by this, we propose LLM-sEMG, a novel framework that leverages LLMs as sEMG activity recognizers. Within this framework, we design a language-oriented mapping mechanism that converts continuous sEMG sequences into sEMG language, integrating several strategies to further facilitate the signal-to-language mapping process. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves highly accurate sEMG signal-based activity recognition using large language models.

中文摘要翻译: 基于表面肌电信号（sEMG）的活动识别近年来受到越来越多研究关注。为了构建准确的基于sEMG信号的活动识别器，研究者提出了众多方法。一些研究专注于设计更大、更具表达力的模型架构以增强sEMG信号的表示能力，而另一些研究则旨在通过大规模预训练丰富模型先验，从而提升识别性能。近期，大语言模型（LLMs）在自然语言处理领域展现出了卓越的泛化与推理能力，其从大量动作语言描述中学到的隐含知识，为解读sEMG信号和推断活动意图开辟了新的可能性。受此启发，我们提出LLM-sEMG，一个利用大语言模型作为sEMG活动识别器的新颖框架。在该框架内，我们设计了一种语言导向的映射机制，将连续的sEMG序列转换为sEMG语言，并集成了多种策略以进一步促进信号到语言的映射过程。大量实验表明，所提框架利用大语言模型实现了基于sEMG信号的高精度活动识别。

</details>


### [69] [From Recognition to Reasoning: Benchmarking and Enhancing MLLMs on Real-World Receipt Document Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22413)
*Yandi Wang,Libin Zhan,Ziwei Huang,Tiancheng Luo,Yuxuan Jiang,Wang Dong,Leilei Gan,Jun Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了ReceiptBench，一个包含1万张人工标注收据的大规模基准，用于视觉文档信息提取，并设计了四层递进子任务和基于GRPO的两阶段训练框架，在复杂推理任务上超越了领先的商业模型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有VIE基准在规模、真实性和语义粒度上存在严重不足，且未能覆盖多样化的文档类型，亟需更全面、更具挑战性的评测基准。

Method: 提出ReceiptBench基准（10k收据，四层递进子任务）和两阶段训练框架，其中Metric-Aware GRPO将严格的评估约束转化为强化学习信号，以增强结构一致性。

Result: 所提方法在复杂推理任务上取得最先进性能，超越了包括领先商业模型在内的现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过大规模、细粒度的基准和面向结构一致性的强化学习训练框架，有效提升了视觉文档信息提取在复杂场景下的性能。

Original Abstract: Extracting structured information from visual documents (Visual Information Extraction, VIE) is a cornerstone of business automation. While recent Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have shown promising capabilities, existing benchmarks suffer from critical limitations in scale and realism, lack semantic granularity, and fail to cover diverse document types. To bridge this gap, we introduce ReceiptBench, a large-scale, human-annotated benchmark consisting of 10k diverse receipts, organizing information extraction into four hierarchical sub-tasks: (1) Basic Perception for raw text spotting, (2) Format Normalization for strictly following standardization instructions, (3) Semantic Reasoning for inferring implicit attributes from context, and (4) Structure Parsing for handling nested line items. Furthermore, we propose a two-stage training framework incorporating Metric-Aware Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), which translates rigorous evaluation constraints into reinforcement learning signals to enhance structural consistency. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method yields state-of-the-art performance, surpassing leading proprietary models on complex reasoning tasks. We release our datasets and code at https://github.com/wwwT0ri/ReceiptBench.

中文摘要翻译: 从视觉文档中提取结构化信息（视觉信息提取，VIE）是业务自动化的基石。尽管最新的多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）已展现出有前景的能力，但现有基准在规模和真实性方面存在严重局限性，缺乏语义粒度，且未能覆盖多样化的文档类型。为弥补这一差距，我们提出了ReceiptBench，一个大规模、人工标注的基准，包含1万张多样化的收据，将信息提取组织为四个递进子任务：（1）基础感知——原始文本定位；（2）格式规范化——严格遵循标准化指令；（3）语义推理——从上下文中推断隐式属性；（4）结构解析——处理嵌套的行项目。此外，我们提出了一个两阶段训练框架，结合了度量感知的组相对策略优化（GRPO），将严格的评估约束转化为强化学习信号，以增强结构一致性。大量实验表明，我们的方法取得了最先进的性能，在复杂推理任务上超越了领先的商业模型。我们在https://github.com/wwwT0ri/ReceiptBench上发布数据集和代码。

</details>


### [70] [Towards Clinically Interpretable Ophthalmic VQA via Spatially-Grounded Lesion Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22414)
*Xingyue Wang,Bo Liu,Meng Wang,Zhixuan Zhang,Chengcheng Zhu,Huazhu Fu,Jiang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了FundusGround，一个面向临床可解释眼科VQA的新基准，通过ETDRS网格对病灶进行空间定位，构建了包含10,719张眼底图像、15,595个病灶标注和72,706个问题的数据集，实验表明显式空间定位的病灶级视觉证据能提升模型性能和透明度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有眼科VQA基准过度强调答案准确率，忽视了临床可解释性所需的显式视觉证据，缺乏对病灶的空间定位能力。

Method: 提出三阶段流程：(1) 收集10,719张眼底图像并精细化标注15,595个病灶；(2) 利用ETDRS网格将病灶空间定位到9个临床意义的视网膜区域；(3) 基于结构化病灶证据生成72,706个四类格式问题（开放、封闭、单选、多选），并用双指标评估答案准确性和病灶级推理能力。

Result: 多个通用和医学大视觉语言模型的实验表明，引入病灶级视觉证据一致性地提升了模型性能和透明度，证明了显式空间定位对可靠可解释眼科VQA的必要性。

Conclusion: 显式空间定位的病灶级视觉证据是构建可靠且可解释的眼科VQA系统的关键。

Original Abstract: Visual Question Answering (VQA) holds great promise for clinical support, particularly in ophthalmology, where retinal fundus photography is essential for diagnosis. However, ophthalmic VQA benchmarks primarily emphasize answer accuracy, neglecting the explicit visual evidence necessary for clinical interpretability. In this work, we introduce FundusGround, a new benchmark for clinically interpretable ophthalmic VQA with spatially-grounded lesion evidence. Specifically, we propose a three-stage pipeline that collects 10,719 fundus images with 15,595 image-level meticulously annotated lesions. To ensure anatomical consistency and clinical validity, all lesions are spatially localized using the Early Treatment Diabetic Retinopathy Study (ETDRS) grid, enabling standardized mapping to nine clinically meaningful retinal regions. Built upon this structured lesion evidence, 72,706 questions are then generated spanning four formats: open-ended, closed-ended, single-choice, and multiple-choice. We further benchmark multiple general- and medical- large vision-language models using dual metrics for answer accuracy and lesion-level reasoning. The experiments demonstrate that incorporating lesion-level visual evidence consistently improves model performance and transparency, highlighting the necessity of explicit spatial grounding for reliable and explainable ophthalmic VQA.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉问答（VQA）在临床支持方面前景广阔，尤其是在以视网膜眼底摄影为诊断核心的眼科学领域。然而，现有的眼科VQA基准主要强调答案准确率，忽视了临床可解释性所需的显式视觉证据。本文提出了FundusGround——一个基于空间定位病灶证据的临床可解释眼科VQA新基准。具体而言，我们设计了一个三阶段流程，收集了10,719张眼底图像并包含15,595个经过图像级精细标注的病灶。为确保解剖一致性和临床有效性，所有病灶均使用早期治疗糖尿病视网膜病变研究（ETDRS）网格进行空间定位，从而实现向九个临床意义的视网膜区域标准化映射。基于这些结构化病灶证据，生成了72,706个问题，涵盖开放型、封闭型、单选和多选四种格式。我们进一步使用答案准确率和病灶级推理双指标，对多个通用和医学大视觉语言模型进行了基准测试。实验结果表明，引入病灶级视觉证据一致性地提升了模型性能和透明度，凸显了显式空间定位对于构建可靠且可解释的眼科VQA系统的必要性。

</details>


### [71] [The Neglected Baseline in Model Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22417)
*Yongjin Cui,Xiaohui Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出现有模型解释方法普遍忽略基线(baseline)的问题，重新阐述了基线在模型解释中的重要性，统一了多种梯度类方法的理论框架，揭示了各方法的基线，分析了现有方法的缺陷，并提出了一个具有清晰合理基线的改进模型解释方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有模型解释方法普遍忽略基线，导致解释结果不精确甚至错误。作者希望通过重新阐述基线的重要性，统一相关方法的理论框架，并纠正现有方法在基线和评估准则上的缺陷。

Method: 1) 重新阐述模型解释任务和解释原则，强调基线的重要性；2) 统一梯度方法、积分梯度(IG)方法和泰勒展开，明确各方法的基线；3) 分析IG、LayerCAM、ODAM、Difference Map等方法中由基线问题导致的缺陷；4) 提出用归因误差(attribution error)替代基于边际效应或完美模型假设的评估方法；5) 修正IG方法，提出具有清晰合理基线的模型解释方法，支持基于任意层特征的解释。

Result: 改进后的方法在解释质量上优于现有方法；基于不同层特征的解释都是合理的，其差异反映了不同特征提取阶段的程度差异。

Conclusion: 基线在模型解释中至关重要，统一了多种梯度类方法的理论框架后可以揭示各方法的基线问题。通过修正基线，可以获得更精确的模型解释结果，且该方法支持基于任意层特征的解释，不同层的解释结果具有不同的语义层次。

Original Abstract: We observe that existing model interpretation methods generally ignore the baseline, and such neglect often results in imprecise or even incorrect interpretation. In this paper, we reformulate the task of model interpretation and the interpretation principles for model interpretation results to demonstrate the importance of the baseline. We further unify gradient-based methods, Integrated Gradients (IG) methods, and Taylor expansion, clarifying the connections among them and explicitly identifying the baseline for each method. On this basis, we analyze the flaws and errors in related model interpretation methods (IG, LayerCAM, ODAM, Difference Map). We advocate evaluating the quality of model interpretation results precisely through the attribution error between the attribution result and the attribution target, rather than adopting flawed evaluation methods, such as those based on marginal-effect or the assumption of perfect model performance. We revise IG and develope a model interpretation method with a clear and reasonable baseline, achieving better results. Our method supports model interpretation based on features from any layer. Interpretation based on features from different layers are all reasonable, and the differences among these results reflect varying degrees of feature extraction at different feature extraction stages.

中文摘要翻译: 我们观察到现有的模型解释方法普遍忽略了基线(baseline)，这种忽略往往导致解释结果不精确甚至错误。在本文中，我们重新阐述了模型解释任务以及模型解释结果的解释原则，以证明基线的重要性。我们进一步统一了基于梯度的方法、积分梯度(Integrated Gradients, IG)方法和泰勒展开，阐明了它们之间的联系并明确指出了每种方法的基线。在此基础上，我们分析了相关模型解释方法（IG、LayerCAM、ODAM、Difference Map）中的缺陷与错误。我们主张通过归因结果与归因目标之间的归因误差(attribution error)来精确评估模型解释结果的质量，而不是采用有缺陷的评估方法（例如基于边际效应或基于完美模型性能假设的方法）。我们修正了IG方法，并开发了一种具有清晰合理基线的模型解释方法，取得了更好的结果。我们的方法支持基于任意层特征的解释。基于不同层特征的解释都是合理的，这些结果的差异反映了不同特征提取阶段中特征提取程度的差异。

</details>


### [72] [Diffusion-guided Generalizable Enhancer for Urban Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22420)
*Henry Che,Jingkang Wang,Yun Chen,Ze Yang,Sivabalan Manivasagam,Raquel Urtasun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GenRe提出了一种扩散引导的通用增强器，能够在几分钟内修复预训练3D高斯表示的缺陷，实现高效、鲁棒且可泛化的城市场景重建，显著提升大视角偏移下的渲染质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有神经渲染方法在记录轨迹上效果很好，但大视角偏移下质量严重下降；近期扩散模型方法虽能改善，但需要昂贵的逐场景优化且泛化能力差，无法扩展到有限合成视图之外。

Method: GenRe是一个扩散引导的通用增强器，输入任意预训练的3D高斯表示，通过学习跨场景的生成先验蒸馏，在几分钟内修复表示缺陷，生成鲁棒且高保真的3D表示。

Result: 实验表明，GenRe在质量和效率上均优于现有方法，并能有效支持多种下游任务，如变道等挑战性未见视角下实现鲁棒且可扩展的传感器仿真。

Conclusion: GenRe通过跨场景学习扩散先验蒸馏，实现了高效、鲁棒且可泛化的城市场景重建，为自动驾驶闭环仿真提供了实用解决方案。

Original Abstract: Urban scene reconstruction from real-world observations has emerged as a powerful tool for self-driving development and testing. While current neural rendering approaches achieve high-fidelity rendering along the recorded trajectories, their quality degrades significantly under large viewpoint shifts, limiting the applicability for closed-loop simulation. Recent works have shown promising results in using diffusion models to enhance quality at these challenging viewpoints and distill improvements back into 3D representations. However, they often require costly per-scene optimization, and the distilled representations remain fragile and fail to generalize beyond limited synthesized views. To address these limitations, we propose GenRe, a novel diffusion-guided generalizable enhancer for urban scene reconstruction. GenRe takes as input any pretrained 3D Gaussian representation and fixes the deficiencies within a few minutes. By learning to distill generative priors across diverse scenes, GenRe produces robust and high-fidelity representation efficiently that generalizes reliably to challenging unseen viewpoints (e.g., lane change). Experiments show that GenRe outperforms existing methods in both quality and efficiency and benefits various downstream tasks, enabling robust and scalable sensor simulation for autonomous driving.

中文摘要翻译: 从真实世界观测中进行城市场景重建已成为自动驾驶开发和测试的强大工具。虽然当前的神经渲染方法在记录轨迹上实现了高保真渲染，但在大视角偏移下其质量显著下降，限制了其在闭环仿真中的适用性。近期研究在利用扩散模型提升这些挑战性视角下的质量并将改进蒸馏回3D表示方面取得了令人鼓舞的成果。然而，这些方法通常需要昂贵的逐场景优化，且蒸馏得到的表示仍然脆弱，无法泛化到有限合成视图之外。为了解决这些局限性，我们提出了GenRe，一种新颖的扩散引导的通用城市场景重建增强器。GenRe以任意预训练的3D高斯表示作为输入，在几分钟内修复其缺陷。通过学习跨不同场景蒸馏生成先验，GenRe高效地生成鲁棒且高保真的表示，能够可靠地泛化到具有挑战性的未见视角（例如变道）。实验表明，GenRe在质量和效率上均优于现有方法，并有益于多种下游任务，为自动驾驶实现了鲁棒且可扩展的传感器仿真。

</details>


### [73] [FastTab: A Fast Table Recognizer with a Tiny Recursive Module and 1D Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22422)
*Laziz Hamdi,Amine Tamasna,Pascal Boisson,Thierry Paquet*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FastTab是一个基于网格的表格结构识别模型，通过轻量级TRM模块和轴向1D Transformer编码器实现低延迟推理，在多个基准上达到竞争性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有TSR方法依赖自回归HTML解码，效率低下；需要同时保持表格级别的整体一致性（行列计数、表头、跨单元格）和精确的分隔线定位。

Method: 提出FastTab模型，包含：(i) 轻量级Tiny Recursive Module (TRM)进行全局推理；(ii) 轴向1D Transformer编码器捕获行列长程依赖；预测行列计数、表头行和分隔线构建网格，再利用ROI对齐的单元格特征推断跨行/跨列。

Result: 在PubTabNet、FinTabNet、PubTables-1M和SciTSR四个基准上达到竞争性的结构恢复性能，同时实现低延迟推理；还研究了像素级匿名化下的鲁棒性，并扩展到弯曲分隔线场景。

Conclusion: FastTab通过避免自回归解码的网格中心方法，在保持高精度结构识别的同时显著降低了推理延迟，并展示了良好的鲁棒性和扩展性。

Original Abstract: Table structure recognition (TSR) requires both table-level coherence (row/column counts, headers, spanning cells) and precise separator localization. We introduce FastTab, a grid-centric TSR model that avoids autoregressive HTML decoding by combining (i) a lightweight Tiny Recursive Module (TRM) for global reasoning and (ii) axial 1D Transformer encoders that capture long-range dependencies along rows and columns. The model predicts row/column counts, header rows, and separators to construct a grid, then infers rowspan/colspan using ROI-aligned cell features. Across four benchmarks (PubTabNet, FinTabNet, PubTables-1M, and SciTSR), FastTab achieves competitive structure recovery performance while operating at low-latency inference. We further study robustness under pixel-level anonymisation and show an extension to curved separators for camera-captured documents. The source code will be made publicly available at https://github.com/hamdilaziz/FastTab .

中文摘要翻译: 表格结构识别（TSR）既需要表格级别的整体一致性（行/列计数、表头、跨单元格），也需要精确的分隔线定位。我们提出FastTab，一种以网格为中心的TSR模型，通过结合(i)用于全局推理的轻量级Tiny Recursive Module (TRM)和(ii)沿行和列方向捕获长程依赖关系的轴向1D Transformer编码器，避免了自回归HTML解码。该模型预测行列计数、表头行和分隔线以构建网格，然后利用ROI对齐的单元格特征推断跨行/跨列。在四个基准数据集（PubTabNet、FinTabNet、PubTables-1M和SciTSR）上，FastTab在实现低延迟推理的同时取得了具有竞争力的结构恢复性能。我们进一步研究了模型在像素级匿名化下的鲁棒性，并展示了向相机拍摄文档中弯曲分隔线的扩展。源代码将在https://github.com/hamdilaziz/FastTab 公开提供。

</details>


### [74] [Making the Discrete Continuous: Synthetic RAW Augmentations for Fine-Grained Evaluation of Person Detection Performance in Low Light](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22455)
*Valeria Pais,Malena Mendilaharzu,Daniele Faccio,Luis Oala,Christoph Clausen,Bruno Sanguinetti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究如何利用合成低光数据增强AI视觉模型在夜间行人检测中的性能评估，通过RAW图像增强技术生成匹配传感器噪声模型的合成数据，实验表明模型难以区分真实与合成低光数据。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现实数据集存在稀疏、不均衡（长尾分布）的问题，限制了AI视觉模型的泛化能力和评估可靠性，尤其是在自动驾驶安全关键的夜间行人检测场景中，低光照区域的真实样本稀缺。

Method: 采用合成RAW图像增强技术，生成与相机传感器噪声模型相匹配的低光样本，以更连续地采样输入空间，提升基准测试的数据覆盖度。

Result: 在真实与合成低光数据上的性能指标相似，表明AI模型难以区分真实与合成低光样本。

Conclusion: 合成数据可以有效填补真实数据在低密度区域的空白，用于更好地刻画模型在不同光照条件下的性能，且合成数据的保真度足够高以近似真实场景。

Original Abstract: Real-world deployment of AI vision models is both fueled and limited by the data available for training and testing. Real datasets are sparse and uneven: long-tailed or unbalanced distributions hinder generalization, and the low number of samples in low density regions makes it hard to run evaluations. Synthetic data can fill these gaps, providing us with a way to sample the input space more continuously and improve data coverage for benchmarks. Focusing on the autonomous driving safety-critical case of pedestrian detection in the dark, we show how synthetic low-light samples can be used to better characterize the performance of a state-of-the-art object detection model as a function of the scene illumination. We use a synthetic RAW image augmentation technique to generate low-light samples that match the noise model of the camera sensor. Performance metrics on real and synthetic low-light data are similar, indicating that the AI model finds it hard to distinguish between them.

中文摘要翻译: AI视觉模型在实际场景中的部署既受训练和测试可用数据的推动，也受其限制。真实数据集稀疏且不均匀：长尾或不平衡分布阻碍了泛化，低密度区域的样本数量少使得评估困难。合成数据可以填补这些空白，为我们提供一种更连续地采样输入空间的方法，并提升基准测试的数据覆盖度。聚焦于夜间行人检测这一自动驾驶安全关键场景，我们展示了如何利用合成低光样本来更好地刻画最先进目标检测模型在不同场景光照下的性能。我们采用一种合成RAW图像增强技术，生成与相机传感器噪声模型相匹配的低光样本。在真实与合成低光数据上的性能指标相似，表明AI模型难以区分两者。

</details>


### [75] [MaSC: A Masked Similarity Metric for Evaluating Concept-Driven Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22469)
*Patryk Bartkowiak,Lennart Petersen,Bartosz Kotrys,Dominik Michels,Soren Pirk,Wojtek Palubicki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MaSC指标，通过前景掩码将评估分解为概念保持（基于掩码最大余弦匹配）和提示遵循（基于背景池化嵌入），在DreamBench++和ORIDa上优于现有全局指标，接近GPT-4o水平。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有评估指标（如CLIP-I、DINO、CLIP-T）使用全局图像或文本-图像嵌入，无法将概念主体与背景分离，导致与人类感知相关性差。

Method: MaSC利用外部提供的前景概念掩码，将评估分解为：1）概念保持：通过冻结的SigLIP2 SO400M-NaFlex特征，计算前景参考块与生成图像块之间的掩码最大余弦匹配；2）提示遵循：通过比较仅背景的池化图像嵌入与去除主体的提示嵌入来衡量。

Result: 在DreamBench++上，概念保持的Krippendorff alpha=0.471，优于所有非LLM基线及GPT-4V，接近GPT-4o；在ORIDa上AUC=0.992，近乎完美区分同主体与跨主体对；提示遵循分数也优于DreamBench++自带的CLIP-T基线。

Conclusion: 空间分解聚合是评估概念驱动生成任务的一个强设计原则。

Original Abstract: Evaluating single-concept personalization in text-to-image diffusion requires measuring both concept preservation, which captures identity fidelity to a reference, and prompt following, which captures whether the generated scene matches the prompt. Existing metrics commonly compute these signals using global image or text-image embeddings, such as CLIP-I, DINO, and CLIP-T. We show that such metrics correlate poorly with human perception because they attend to the image as a whole instead of separating the concept subject from the background. We introduce MaSC, a masked similarity metric that uses externally provided foreground concept masks to decompose evaluation into subject-specific concept preservation and background-based prompt following. MaSC computes both scores from frozen SigLIP2 SO400M-NaFlex features: concept preservation is measured by masked max-cosine matching between foreground reference patches and generated-image patches, while prompt following is measured by comparing a background-only pooled image embedding to a subject-stripped prompt embedding. On DreamBench++ human ratings, MaSC achieves Krippendorff alpha = 0.471 for concept preservation, outperforming all tested non-LLM baselines and GPT-4V, and approaching GPT-4o. On ORIDa, a real-photo identity-preservation benchmark across physical environments, MaSC achieves AUC = 0.992, nearly perfectly distinguishing same-subject from cross-subject pairs. Its prompt-following score also outperforms the CLIP-T baseline shipped with DreamBench++. These results show that spatially decomposed aggregation is a strong design principle for evaluating concept-driven generation.

中文摘要翻译: 评估文生图扩散模型中的单一概念个性化，需要同时衡量概念保持（即对参考图像的身份保真度）和提示遵循（即生成场景与提示的匹配程度）。现有指标通常使用全局图像或文本-图像嵌入（如CLIP-I、DINO和CLIP-T）来计算这些信号。我们发现，此类指标与人类感知的相关性较差，因为它们将图像作为整体来关注，而非将概念主体与背景分离。我们提出了MaSC——一种掩码相似度指标，利用外部提供的前景概念掩码将评估分解为基于主体的概念保持和基于背景的提示遵循。MaSC从冻结的SigLIP2 SO400M-NaFlex特征中计算两种分数：概念保持通过前景参考块与生成图像块之间的掩码最大余弦匹配来衡量；提示遵循则通过比较仅背景的池化图像嵌入与去除主体的提示嵌入来衡量。在DreamBench++的人类评分上，MaSC的概念保持分数达到Krippendorff alpha=0.471，优于所有测试的非大语言模型基线以及GPT-4V，并接近GPT-4o。在ORIDa（一个跨物理环境的真实照片身份保持基准）上，MaSC取得了AUC=0.992，近乎完美地区分同主体对与跨主体对。其提示遵循分数也优于DreamBench++自带的CLIP-T基线。这些结果表明，空间分解聚合是评估概念驱动生成任务的一个强设计原则。

</details>


### [76] [Matching with Deliberation: Test-Time Evolutionary Hierarchical Multi-Agents for Zero-Shot Compositional Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22478)
*Xingtian Pei,Yukun Song,Changwei Wang,Shunpeng Chen,Rongtao Xu,Shibiao Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种分层感知-决策框架（PDF），通过多智能体架构和经验自演化机制解决零样本组合图像检索中的感知短视和逻辑漂移问题，在三个基准数据集上取得SOTA性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有ZS-CIR方法存在单空间感知短视（Perception Myopia）或迭代协作中因底层检索器感知上限导致的逻辑漂移（Logic Drift）问题，亟需一种能够兼顾视觉连续性保持与语义变量忠实执行的统一框架。

Method: 提出分层感知-决策框架（PDF）：(1) 意图路由管理器（Intent Routing Manager）根据修改意图动态调度多视角Worker感知信号，构建高召回候选池；(2) 决策管理器（Decision Manager）结合无训练推理策略蒸馏（Training-free Reasoning Policy Distillation）和锦标赛式测试时缩放策略（Tournament-style TTS），实现自演化的细粒度推理。

Result: 在CIRR、CIRCO和FashionIQ三个基准数据集上均达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 经验驱动的自演化和测试时缩放策略（TTS）是实现零样本细粒度多媒体检索的一条极具前景且可扩展的路径。

Original Abstract: Zero-Shot Compositional Image Retrieval (ZS-CIR) requires both preserving the visual continuity of the reference image and faithfully executing the semantic variables specified in the modification text, which constitutes the core challenge of the task. Existing methods often suffer from Perception Myopia in a single space, or fall into Logic Drift in iterative collaboration due to the perception ceiling of the underlying retriever. To address this issue, we propose a one-stop hierarchical Perception-to-Deliberation Framework (PDF), which, to the best of our knowledge, is the first to introduce experience self-evolution and Test-Time Scaling Law (TTS) into ZS-CIR. Relying on a hierarchical multi-agent architecture, PDF first utilizes an Intent Routing Manager to dynamically dispatch multi-view Worker perception signals based on modification intents to construct a high-recall candidate pool. Subsequently, the Decision Manager combines a Training-free Reasoning Policy Distillation mechanism with a Tournament-style TTS strategy to achieve self-evolving fine-grained reasoning, yielding the final retrieval results. Experimental results demonstrate that PDF achieves SOTA performance on three benchmark datasets: CIRR, CIRCO, and FashionIQ. This study indicates that experience-driven self-evolution and TTS represent a highly promising and scalable path for achieving zero-shot fine-grained multimedia retrieval. The code will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

中文摘要翻译: 零样本组合图像检索（ZS-CIR）要求在保留参考图像视觉连续性的同时，忠实地执行修改文本中指定的语义变量，这构成了该任务的核心挑战。现有方法常常陷入单一空间中的感知短视，或因底层检索器的感知上限而在迭代协作中产生逻辑漂移。为解决这一问题，我们提出了一种一站式分层感知-决策框架（PDF），据我们所知，这是首次将经验自演化和测试时缩放策略（TTS）引入ZS-CIR。依赖分层多智能体架构，PDF首先利用意图路由管理器根据修改意图动态调度多视角Worker感知信号，构建高召回候选池。随后，决策管理器将无训练推理策略蒸馏机制与锦标赛式TTS策略相结合，实现自演化的细粒度推理，得出最终检索结果。实验结果表明，PDF在三个基准数据集CIRR、CIRCO和FashionIQ上均取得了SOTA性能。本研究表明，经验驱动的自演化和TTS是实现零样本细粒度多媒体检索的一条极具前景且可扩展的路径。代码将在论文被接收后公开发布。

</details>


### [77] [Supervised Classification Heads as Semantic Prototypes: Unlocking Vision-Language Alignment via Weight Recycling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22484)
*David Méndez,Roberto Confalonieri,Natalia Díaz Rodríguez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现视觉模型的分类头权重可重用为语义原型，既能作为零样本对齐的语义锚点，也能作为数据增强策略与真实图文对混合，从而提升后对齐方法在多模态任务上的表现。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视觉-语言模型需要昂贵的端到端训练和大量配对数据，而后期对齐方法虽降低了计算成本，但仍依赖大量配对数据。作者希望利用预训练视觉模型中通常被丢弃的分类头权重，挖掘其作为语义原型的潜力，以减少对配对数据的依赖。

Method: 将预训练视觉模型的分类头权重回收利用为语义原型：一方面作为语义锚点实现零样本对齐，另一方面作为数据增强策略，将这些原型与真实图像-文本对混合，再集成到现有的后期对齐方法中进行训练。

Result: 该方法与多种先进的后期对齐技术结合后，在跨模态检索、零样本和小样本分类任务上持续提升了准确率。

Conclusion: 回收利用预训练视觉模型的分类头权重是一种简单有效且通用的策略，能够显著增强后期对齐方法在多模态理解任务上的性能。

Original Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) excel at tasks like zero-shot classification and cross-modal retrieval by mapping images and text to a shared space, but this requires expensive end-to-end training with massive paired datasets. Current post-hoc alignment methods reduce computational costs by connecting pretrained encoders through lightweight mappings, yet still demand substantial paired data. In this work, we investigate the potential of repurposing the classification heads of pretrained vision models as semantic prototypes. The recycling of these weights, typically discarded after pretraining, unlocks two distinct capabilities: it enables zero-shot alignment by using weights as semantic anchors, and serves as a robust data augmentation strategy by mixing these prototypes with real image-text pairs. We demonstrate that integrating our approach with several state-of-the-art post-hoc alignment techniques consistently boosts accuracy in cross-modal retrieval, zero- and few-shot classification tasks.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉-语言模型通过将图像和文本映射到共享空间，在零样本分类和跨模态检索等任务上表现出色，但这需要利用大规模配对数据集进行昂贵的端到端训练。现有的后期对齐方法通过轻量级映射连接预训练编码器来降低计算成本，但仍然需要大量配对数据。在这项工作中，我们探索了将预训练视觉模型的分类头重新用作语义原型的潜力。这些通常在预训练后被丢弃的权重被回收利用，解锁了两种不同的能力：通过将权重作为语义锚点实现零样本对齐，以及将这些原型与真实图像-文本对混合作为鲁棒的数据增强策略。我们证明，将我们的方法与多种先进的后期对齐技术相结合，能够在跨模态检索、零样本和小样本分类任务上持续提升准确率。

</details>


### [78] [Training-Free Fine-Grained Semantic Segmentations in Low Data Regimes: A FungiTastic Baseline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22492)
*Sebastian Cavada,Francesco Pelosin,Lapo Faggi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需训练的两阶段框架，将语义分割中的分割与分类解耦，利用SAM3进行粗粒度掩码生成、DINOv3进行细粒度原型匹配分类，在蘑菇细粒度分割的低数据场景下取得了首个基线结果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 细粒度语义分割既需要精确定位，又需要区分视觉相似的类别。在FungiTastic数据集中，长尾分布和图像采集条件的强烈变化使问题更加复杂。现有方法难以在低数据场景下兼顾分割的可扩展性和分类精度。

Method: 提出一种无需训练的两阶段框架：第一阶段，SAM3利用宏观分类提示生成类别无关的蘑菇掩码；第二阶段，DINOv3通过嵌入空间中的原型匹配分配细粒度标签，并对DINOv3特征空间进行简单变换以改进原型分类性能。

Result: 在从单样本到数百样本的低数据场景下报告了结果，提供了细粒度语义分割在低数据设置下的首个基线。

Conclusion: 与类别特定提示方法相比，该方法更具可扩展性且分割成本低，为低数据条件下的细粒度语义分割提供了有效的基线方案。

Original Abstract: Fine-grained semantic segmentation requires both precise localization and discrimination between visually similar classes. In FungiTastic, this problem is further complicated by a long-tailed distribution and strong variation in image acquisition conditions. We propose a training-free two-stage framework that decouples segmentation from classification. SAM3 first produces class-agnostic mushroom masks using macro-taxonomic prompts, and DINOv3 then assigns fine-grained labels through prototype matching in the embedding space. To improve this stage, we apply a simple transformation of the DINOv3 feature space that improves prototype-based classification. Compared with class-specific prompting, our approach is more scalable and keeps the segmentation cost low. We report results from one-shot to few-hundred-shot regimes, providing, to the best of our knowledge, the first baseline for fine-grained semantic segmentation in low-data settings.

中文摘要翻译: 细粒度语义分割既需要精确定位，也需要区分视觉上相似的类别。在FungiTastic数据集中，长尾分布和图像采集条件的强烈变化使这一问题更加复杂。我们提出了一种无需训练的两阶段框架，将分割与分类解耦。SAM3首先利用宏观分类提示生成类别无关的蘑菇掩码，然后DINOv3通过嵌入空间中的原型匹配分配细粒度标签。为改进这一阶段，我们对DINOv3特征空间进行了简单变换，提升了基于原型的分类性能。与类别特定提示方法相比，我们的方法更具可扩展性，且分割成本较低。我们报告了从单样本到数百样本场景下的结果，据我们所知，这是首个在低数据设置下针对细粒度语义分割的基线方法。

</details>


### [79] [SpaceDG: Benchmarking Spatial Intelligence under Visual Degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22536)
*Xiaolong Zhou,Yifei Liu,Ziyang Gong,Jiarui Li,Qiyue Zhao,Muyao Niu,Yuanyuan Gao,Le Ma,Xue Yang,Hongjie Zhang,Zhihang Zhong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建了首个面向视觉退化感知的空间理解数据集SpaceDG和基准SpaceDG-Bench，系统评估了25个多模态大模型在运动模糊、低光照等9种退化下的空间推理鲁棒性，发现退化会严重损害模型性能，而基于SpaceDG的微调能显著提升鲁棒性甚至超越人类表现。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有空间推理基准大多假设完美视觉输入，忽视了实际部署中常见的视觉退化（如运动模糊、低光照、恶劣天气、镜头畸变、压缩伪影等），因此需要探究当前多模态大模型在非完美视觉观测下的空间智能鲁棒性。

Method: 构建了物理驱动的退化合成引擎，将退化形成过程嵌入3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）渲染中，模拟9种退化类型；基于近1000个室内场景生成约100万QA对；并构建了人工验证的基准SpaceDG-Bench，涵盖11个推理类别和9种退化类型，共1102道题，超过1万VQA实例。

Result: 对25个开源和闭源MLLM的评估表明，视觉退化一致且显著地损害了空间推理能力，暴露了关键的鲁棒性差距；在SpaceDG上微调可显著提升退化鲁棒性，在退化条件下甚至超越人类表现，且在干净图像上无性能下降。

Conclusion: 退化感知训练对于实现鲁棒的空间智能具有重要前景，SpaceDG数据集和基准为相关研究提供了基础。

Original Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have made rapid progress in spatial intelligence, yet existing spatial reasoning benchmarks largely assume pristine visual inputs and overlook the degradations that commonly occur in real-world deployment, such as motion blur, low light, adverse weather, lens distortion, and compression artifacts. This raises a fundamental question: how robust is the spatial intelligence of current MLLMs when visual observations are imperfect? To answer this question, we introduce SpaceDG, the first large-scale dataset for degradation-aware spatial understanding. It is constructed with a physically grounded degradation synthesis engine that embeds degradation formation process into 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) rendering, enabling realistic simulation of nine degradation types. The resulting dataset contains approximately 1M QA pairs from nearly 1,000 indoor scenes. We further introduce SpaceDG-Bench, an human-verified benchmark with 1,102 questions spanning 11 reasoning categories and 9 visual degradation types, yielding over 10K VQA instances. Evaluating 25 open- and closed-source MLLMs reveals that visual degradations consistently and substantially impair spatial reasoning, exposing a critical robustness gap. Finally, we show that finetuning on SpaceDG markedly improves degradation robustness and can even surpass human performance under degraded conditions without any performance drop on clean images, highlighting the promise of degradation-aware training for robust spatial intelligence.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态大语言模型（MLLM）在空间智能方面取得了快速进展，然而现有的空间推理基准大多假设视觉输入是完美的，忽略了实际部署中常见的退化问题，例如运动模糊、低光照、恶劣天气、镜头畸变和压缩伪影。这引出了一个根本性问题：当视觉观测不完美时，当前MLLM的空间智能有多鲁棒？为回答这个问题，我们引入了SpaceDG——首个面向退化感知空间理解的大规模数据集。它通过一个物理驱动的退化合成引擎构建，该引擎将退化形成过程嵌入到3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）渲染中，实现了九种退化类型的逼真模拟。生成的数据集包含来自近1000个室内场景的约100万个问答对。我们进一步引入了SpaceDG-Bench，一个人工验证的基准，包含覆盖11个推理类别和9种视觉退化类型的1102个问题，产生超过1万个VQA实例。对25个开源和闭源MLLM的评估揭示，视觉退化一致且显著地损害了空间推理能力，暴露了一个关键的鲁棒性差距。最后，我们表明在SpaceDG上进行微调显著改善了退化鲁棒性，甚至可以在退化条件下超越人类表现，同时在干净图像上没有任何性能下降，这突显了退化感知训练对于实现鲁棒空间智能的前景。

</details>


### [80] [Segment Anything with Motion, Geometry, and Semantic Adaptation for Complex Nonlinear Visual Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22538)
*Deyi Zhu,Yuji Wang,Yong Liu,Yansong Tang,Bingyao Yu,Jiwen Lu,Jie Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SAMOSA框架，通过显式建模运动、几何和语义线索，将SAM 2适配到复杂视觉目标跟踪场景，在多个基准上超越现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: SAM 2虽具备强大的视频理解先验，但直接应用于VOT时不显式建模目标运动动力学，也未强制帧间的几何与语义一致性，导致跟踪性能次优。

Method: 引入轻量级非线性运动预测器建模目标动态、引导掩码选择与记忆过滤；利用语义线索检测目标偏移并恢复跟踪失败；融合几何线索作为结构约束以提升跟踪稳定性。

Result: 在通用基准上持续优于基于SAM 2的现有方法；泛化性强于监督式VOT方法；在反UAV数据集（典型非线性运动场景）上取得显著增益。

Conclusion: SAMOSA通过显式引入运动、几何与语义建模，弥合了SAM 2隐式视频理解先验与显式跟踪建模之间的差距，实现了更鲁棒和泛化的跟踪。

Original Abstract: Traditional visual object tracking (VOT) methods typically rely on task-specific supervised training, limiting their generalization to unseen objects and challenging scenarios with distractors, occlusion, and nonlinear motion. Recent vision foundation models, exemplified by SAM 2, learn strong video understanding priors from large-scale pretraining and offer a promising foundation for building more robust and generalizable trackers. However, directly applying SAM 2 to VOT remains suboptimal, as it does not explicitly model target motion dynamics or enforce geometric and semantic consistency across frames, both of which are essential for reliable tracking. To address this issue, we propose SAMOSA, a new tracking framework that adapts SAM 2 to complex VOT scenarios by explicitly leveraging motion, geometry, and semantic cues. Specifically, we introduce a lightweight nonlinear motion predictor to model target dynamics and guide mask selection as well as memory filtering. We further exploit semantic cues to detect target shifts and recover from tracking failures, while geometric cues are incorporated as structural constraints to improve tracking stability. In this way, SAMOSA bridges the gap between the implicit video understanding prior of SAM 2 and explicit tracking-oriented modeling. Extensive experiments show that SAMOSA consistently outperforms state-of-the-art SAM 2--based approaches on general benchmarks, demonstrates stronger generalization than supervised VOT methods, and achieves substantial gains on anti-UAV datasets, which typify complex nonlinear motion scenarios. Our code is available at https://github.com/DurYi/SAMOSA.

中文摘要翻译: 传统的视觉目标跟踪（VOT）方法通常依赖任务特定的监督训练，限制了其对未见物体以及存在干扰物、遮挡和非线性运动等挑战性场景的泛化能力。近期以SAM 2为代表的视觉基础模型，通过大规模预训练学习了强大的视频理解先验，为构建更鲁棒和更具泛化能力的跟踪器提供了有前景的基础。然而，将SAM 2直接应用于VOT仍非最优，因为它未显式建模目标运动动力学，也未强制帧间的几何与语义一致性，而这两者对于可靠的跟踪至关重要。为解决这一问题，我们提出SAMOSA，一种新的跟踪框架，通过显式利用运动、几何和语义线索将SAM 2适配到复杂的VOT场景。具体而言，我们引入一个轻量级非线性运动预测器来建模目标动态，并引导掩码选择及记忆过滤。我们进一步利用语义线索检测目标偏移并从跟踪失败中恢复，同时将几何线索作为结构约束以提升跟踪稳定性。通过这种方式，SAMOSA弥合了SAM 2隐式视频理解先验与显式跟踪建模之间的差距。大量实验表明，SAMOSA在通用基准上持续优于基于SAM 2的最新方法，展现出比监督式VOT方法更强的泛化能力，并在典型非线性运动场景的反UAV数据集上取得了显著增益。我们的代码已开源至https://github.com/DurYi/SAMOSA。

</details>


### [81] [FashionLens: Toward Versatile Fashion Image Retrieval via Task-Adaptive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22552)
*Haokun Wen,Xuemeng Song,Xinghao Xie,Xiaolin Chen,Xiangyu Zhao,Weili Guan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了FashionLens，一个基于多模态大语言模型的统一时尚图像检索框架，能够处理多种查询格式和搜索意图。通过引入U-FIRE基准数据集、Proposal-Guided Spherical Query Calibrator（动态将查询表示对齐到任务特定的度量空间）和Gradient-Guided Adaptive Sampling策略（基于实时学习难度和数据规模自动重加权任务），在多样化检索场景中实现了最先进的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有时尚图像检索方法专注于狭窄的检索任务，无法充分捕获实际应用中多样化的查询格式和搜索意图，缺乏一个统一框架来支持多种检索场景。

Method: 1) 提出U-FIRE基准数据集，整合碎片化的时尚数据集并补充两个人工标注数据集以测试泛化能力；2) 构建基于多模态大语言模型的FashionLens统一框架；3) 设计Proposal-Guided Spherical Query Calibrator（PSQC），通过自适应球面线性插值将查询表示动态迁移到任务对齐的度量空间；4) 提出Gradient-Guided Adaptive Sampling（GGAS）策略，基于实时学习难度和数据规模自动重加权任务，缓解优化不平衡问题。

Result: 在U-FIRE基准上，FashionLens在多样化检索场景中均达到最先进性能，并对未见过的任务展现出鲁棒的泛化能力。

Conclusion: FashionLens成功实现了统一的多模态时尚图像检索框架，能够处理多样化的真实检索场景，验证了多模态大语言模型在时尚检索领域的潜力。

Original Abstract: Fashion image retrieval is a cornerstone of modern e-commerce systems. A unified framework that supports diverse query formats and search intentions is highly desired in practice. However, existing approaches focus on narrow retrieval tasks and do not fully capture such diversity. Therefore, in this work, we aim to develop a unified framework capable of handling diverse realistic fashion retrieval scenarios, achieving truly versatile fashion image retrieval. To establish a data foundation, we first introduce U-FIRE, a comprehensive benchmark that consolidates fragmented fashion datasets into a unified collection, supplemented by two manually curated datasets for testing generalization. Building upon this, we propose FashionLens, a unified framework based on Multimodal Large Language Models. To handle divergent matching objectives, we design a Proposal-Guided Spherical Query Calibrator that dynamically shifts query representations into task-aligned metric spaces via adaptive spherical linear interpolation. Additionally, to mitigate the optimization imbalance caused by varying task complexities and data scales, we develop a Gradient-Guided Adaptive Sampling strategy that automatically re-weights tasks based on realtime learning difficulty and the data scale prior. Experiments on U-FIRE show that FashionLens achieves state-of-the-art performance across diverse retrieval scenarios and generalizes robustly to unseen tasks. The data and code are publicly released at https://github.com/haokunwen/FashionLens.

中文摘要翻译: 时尚图像检索是现代电子商务系统的基石。一个能够支持多样化查询格式和搜索意图的统一框架在实践中受到高度期待。然而，现有方法聚焦于狭窄的检索任务，未能充分捕捉这种多样性。因此，在本工作中，我们旨在开发一个能够处理多样化真实时尚检索场景的统一框架，实现真正通用的时尚图像检索。为建立数据基础，我们首先引入U-FIRE，一个将碎片化时尚数据集整合为统一集合的综合性基准，并补充了两个人工标注数据集以测试泛化能力。在此基础上，我们提出FashionLens，一个基于多模态大语言模型的统一框架。为处理存在分歧的匹配目标，我们设计了Proposal-Guided Spherical Query Calibrator（提议引导的球面查询校准器），通过自适应球面线性插值将查询表示动态迁移到任务对齐的度量空间。此外，为缓解由不同任务复杂度和数据规模引起的优化不平衡问题，我们开发了Gradient-Guided Adaptive Sampling（梯度引导的自适应采样）策略，基于实时学习难度和数据规模先验自动重加权任务。在U-FIRE上的实验表明，FashionLens在多样化检索场景中达到了最先进的性能，并对未见过的任务展现出鲁棒的泛化能力。数据和代码已在https://github.com/haokunwen/FashionLens公开。

</details>


### [82] [GeoWeaver: Grounding Visual Tokens with Geometric Evidence before Scene Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22558)
*Deshui Miao,Xingsen Huang,Yameng Gu,Xin Li,Haijun Zhang,Ming-Hsuan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoWeaver提出了一种推理前的几何基础框架，通过构建多级几何库并对视觉token进行自适应的几何证据分配，将几何信息作为视觉表征的必要前提，显著提升了视觉-语言模型在时空推理任务中的几何感知能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法虽将几何信息引入多模态模型，但均将几何线索视为所有视觉token共享的全局信号，忽略了不同视觉token因其空间角色不同而需要不同几何证据这一细粒度挑战。

Method: GeoWeaver包含三个关键步骤：(1) 从冻结的几何编码器中构建多级几何库；(2) 对每个视觉token进行自适应的几何证据分配，检索最相关的几何抽象；(3) 通过残差基础操作将选中的几何证据融入视觉token，生成几何基础化的表征用于后续语言建模。

Result: 在多个空间推理基准上的广泛评估表明，GeoWeaver在保持通用多模态能力的同时，一致性地增强了几何感知推理能力。

Conclusion: 几何信息的最佳利用方式并非作为后期融合的辅助信号，而是作为塑造大语言模型推理表征基础的根本性前提。

Original Abstract: Spatio-temporal reasoning in vision-language models requires visual representations that preserve physical geometry rather than merely semantic appearance. Recent multimodal models incorporate geometric information through structural branches, 3D-aware supervision, reasoning-stage fusion, or long-horizon memory. While these approaches demonstrate the importance of geometry for spatial intelligence, they typically treat geometric cues as a shared signal across all visual tokens. We note that this overlooks a finer-grained challenge: different visual tokens require different geometric evidence depending on their spatial roles. To address this limitation, we introduce GeoWeaver, a pre-reasoning geometric grounding framework that treats geometry as a representational prerequisite for spatio-temporal reasoning. GeoWeaver constructs a multi-level geometry bank from a frozen geometry encoder and performs token-adaptive geometric evidence allocation, enabling each visual token to retrieve the most relevant geometric abstractions. The selected evidence is incorporated into visual tokens via a residual grounding operation prior to language modeling, yielding geometry-grounded representations for downstream reasoning. Extensive evaluations on spatial reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that GeoWeaver consistently enhances geometry-aware reasoning while retaining general multimodal capabilities. This indicates that geometric information yields the greatest benefit not as a late-fusion auxiliary signal but as a fundamental prerequisite that shapes the representational foundation on which large language models perform reasoning. All source code and models will be released at https://github.com/yahooo-m/GeoWeaver .

中文摘要翻译: 视觉-语言模型中的时空推理需要能够保留物理几何结构而非仅仅语义外观的视觉表征。近期多模态模型通过结构分支、3D感知监督、推理阶段融合或长程记忆等方式引入几何信息。尽管这些方法证明了几何信息对空间智能的重要性，但它们通常将几何线索视为所有视觉token共享的全局信号。本文指出，这忽略了一个更细粒度的挑战：不同的视觉token根据其空间角色需要不同的几何证据。为解决这一局限，我们提出GeoWeaver——一种推理前的几何基础框架，将几何信息视为时空推理的表征前提。GeoWeaver从冻结的几何编码器中构建多级几何库，并执行token自适应的几何证据分配，使每个视觉token能够检索最相关的几何抽象。选中的证据通过残差基础操作在语言建模之前融入视觉token，从而为下游推理生成几何基础化的表征。在空间推理基准上的广泛评估表明，GeoWeaver在保持通用多模态能力的同时，一致性地增强了几何感知推理能力。这表明几何信息的最佳利用方式并非作为后期融合的辅助信号，而是作为塑造大语言模型推理表征基础的根本性前提。所有源代码和模型将在https://github.com/yahooo-m/GeoWeaver 开源。

</details>


### [83] [VGenST-Bench: A Benchmark for Spatio-Temporal Reasoning via Active Video Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22570)
*Jinho Park,Youbin Kim,Hogun Park,Eunbyung Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VGenST-Bench，一个利用生成模型主动合成多样化视频评估场景的时空推理基准，通过多智能体流程和质量控制构建，支持对多模态大模型进行细粒度诊断。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有时空推理基准数据集主要依赖静态图像集或被动策划的视频数据，限制了细粒度推理能力的评估，亟需一种能够主动合成高度可控且多样化评估场景的新方法。

Method: 提出一个多智能体流程（含人工质量控制阶段）构建VGenST-Bench；建立3×2×2视频分类体系（空间尺度、视角、场景动态）；设计分层任务套件，将低层视觉感知与高层时空推理解耦。

Result: 通过从被动策划转向主动合成，VGenST-Bench能够对多模态大模型的时空理解能力进行细粒度诊断。

Conclusion: VGenST-Bench通过生成模型主动合成视频评估场景，实现了对MLLMs时空推理能力的精确、细粒度评估，为相关领域提供了更有效的基准测试工具。

Original Abstract: Spatio-temporal reasoning is a core capability for Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) operating in the real world. As such, evaluating it precisely has become an essential challenge. However, existing spatio-temporal reasoning benchmark datasets primarily rely on static image sets or passively curated video data, which limits the evaluation of fine-grained reasoning capabilities. In this paper, we introduce VGenST-Bench, a video benchmark that employs generative models to actively synthesize highly controlled and diverse evaluation scenarios. To construct VGenST-Bench, we propose a multi-agent pipeline incorporating a human quality control stage, ensuring the quality of all generated videos and QA pairs. We establish a comprehensive 3x2x2 video taxonomy, encompassing Spatial Scale, Perspective, and Scene Dynamics to span diverse scenarios. Furthermore, we design a hierarchical task suite that decouples low-level visual perception from high-level spatio-temporal reasoning. By shifting the paradigm from passive curation to active synthesis, VGenST-Bench enables fine-grained diagnosis of spatio-temporal understanding in MLLMs.

中文摘要翻译: 时空推理是在现实世界中运行的多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的一项核心能力。因此，精确评估这一能力已成为一项关键挑战。然而，现有的时空推理基准数据集主要依赖于静态图像集或被动策划的视频数据，这限制了细粒度推理能力的评估。在本文中，我们提出了VGenST-Bench，一个采用生成模型主动合成高度可控且多样化评估场景的视频基准。为构建VGenST-Bench，我们提出了一种结合人工质量控制阶段的多智能体流程，以确保所有生成的视频和问答对的质量。我们建立了一个全面的3×2×2视频分类体系，涵盖空间尺度、视角和场景动态三个维度，以覆盖多样化的场景。此外，我们设计了一套分层任务套件，将低层视觉感知与高层时空推理解耦。通过将范式从被动策划转向主动合成，VGenST-Bench能够对多模态大语言模型的时空理解能力进行细粒度诊断。

</details>


### [84] [Beyond Chamfer Distance: Granular Order-aware Evaluation Metric For Online Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22578)
*Chouaib Bencheikh Lehocine,Adam Lilja,Junsheng Fu,Lars Hammarstrand*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对在线地图评估方法中现有度量（基于Chamfer距离的mAP）对点序不敏感、几何质量评估粒度不足的问题，提出了两种新度量：SOSPA（单实例相似度度量，有序感知）和PLD（多实例评估框架，软度量）。在nuScenes上的实验表明，PLD能有效排序SOTA方法并提供分解误差分析，揭示了检测能力是当前方法的主要瓶颈。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有SOTA在线地图方法主要依赖基于阈值化Chamfer距离的mAP进行评估，但该框架缺乏对点序的敏感性，且评估几何质量的粒度有限，难以区分不同方法之间的真实性能差异。

Method: 1) 提出序列最优子模式分配（SOSPA），一种有序感知的单实例相似度度量，满足所有度量公理，支持细粒度几何质量评估。2) 提出折线定位与检测（PLD），一种软度量，将mAP的硬阈值替换为软赋值，联合捕获检测质量和几何精度。

Result: 在nuScenes数据集上，PLD有效排序了MapTRv2、StreamMapNet、MapTracker等SOTA方法，并提供了分解误差分析，揭示了检测能力是当前在线地图方法的主要瓶颈——这一性能趋势是mAP无法捕获的。

Conclusion: 本文提出的SOSPA和PLD度量弥补了现有评估框架的不足，为在线地图估计方法提供了更精细、更公平的评估手段，并指出检测能力是当前方法的关键瓶颈。

Original Abstract: Online map estimation is a crucial component of autonomous driving systems that reduces the reliance on costly high-definition maps. State-of-the-art (SOTA) methods commonly predict map elements as ordered sequences of points that form polylines and polygons. The evaluation of these methods relies predominantly on mean average precision (mAP) based on thresholded Chamfer distance (CD). This framework lacks sensitivity to point ordering and provides limited granularity in assessing geometric quality, making it difficult to distinguish which methods truly excel over others. In this work, we address these limitations on two fronts. For the single-instance similarity measure, we introduce sequence optimal sub-pattern assignment (SOSPA), an order-aware metric that enables fine-grained evaluation of individual geometries while satisfying all metric axioms. For the multi-instance evaluation framework, we propose polyline localisation and detection (PLD), a soft metric that jointly captures detection quality and geometric accuracy, replacing the hard thresholding of mAP with a principled soft assignment. Through evaluations on nuScenes, we demonstrate that PLD effectively ranks SOTA online mapping methods (MapTRv2, StreamMapNet, MapTracker) while providing a decomposed error analysis. This analysis identifies detection capability as the dominant bottleneck in current methods, revealing a performance trend that mAP fails to capture. Code for evaluation using our metrics will be released.

中文摘要翻译: 在线地图估计是自动驾驶系统中减少对昂贵高精地图依赖的关键组成部分。现有最优方法通常将地图元素预测为由点构成的有序序列，形成折线和多边形。这些方法的评估主要依赖于基于阈值化Chamfer距离的均值平均精度（mAP）。该评估框架缺乏对点序的敏感性，且对几何质量的评估粒度有限，难以区分哪些方法真正优于其他方法。本文从两个方面解决这些局限性。对于单实例相似度度量，我们引入了序列最优子模式分配（SOSPA），一种有序感知的度量，能够在满足所有度量公理的同时实现对单个几何结构的细粒度评估。对于多实例评估框架，我们提出了折线定位与检测（PLD），一种软度量，联合捕获检测质量和几何精度，将mAP的硬阈值替换为原则性的软赋值。通过在nuScenes上的评估，我们证明PLD能够有效排序SOTA在线地图方法（MapTRv2、StreamMapNet、MapTracker），同时提供分解误差分析。该分析指出检测能力是当前方法的主要瓶颈，揭示了一个mAP无法捕获的性能趋势。使用我们度量的评估代码将公开发布。

</details>


### [85] [Enhancing Gaze Reasoning in Vision Foundation Models for Gaze Following](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22607)
*Shijing Wang,Yaping Huang,Chaoqun Cui,David Wong,Yihua Cheng,Alexandros Neophytou,Hyung Jin Chang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对VFM在注视跟随任务中缺乏注视推理能力的问题，提出了一种结合头条件局部LoRA和锥外惩罚的训练机制，显著提升了模型在非显著目标上的注视跟随性能，在GazeFollow和VAT数据集上达到SOTA。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于视觉基础模型（VFM）的注视跟随方法虽然大幅提升了场景理解能力，但几乎不贡献注视推理能力，导致模型倾向于依赖语义显著目标而非真实注视线索，当注视目标不显著时性能严重下降。

Method: (1) 头条件局部LoRA（Head-conditioned Local LoRA）：对场景token保持原有学习，对头部token进行局部适配以增强注视推理；(2) 锥外惩罚（Out-of-cone Penalty）：将注视线索注入头部token，同时使头部token与场景token对齐。

Result: 在GazeFollow和VAT数据集上取得SOTA性能，尤其在注视目标不具语义显著性时提升尤为显著。

Conclusion: 该方法有效增强了VFM的注视推理能力，为未来注视跟随研究提供了有价值的见解，代码将在论文接收后开源。

Original Abstract: Gaze following requires both scene understanding and gaze reasoning to localize the gaze target of an in-scene person. Recently, vision foundation models (VFMs) have demonstrated strong performance on this task, enabling simpler architectures while outperforming prior methods. However, we observe a key limitation of VFM-based approaches: while VFMs substantially improve scene understanding, they contribute little to gaze reasoning. As a result, existing methods often rely on semantically salient objects rather than true gaze cues, leading to degraded performance when targets are not salient. To address this, we propose a novel training mechanism to enhance gaze reasoning in VFMs for gaze following. Our method includes: (1) a head-conditioned local LoRA, which enables localized adaptation to preserve scene token learning while improving head token learning for gaze reasoning; and (2) an out-of-cone penalty, which injects gaze cues into head tokens while aligning them with scene tokens. Experiments on the GazeFollow and VAT datasets demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance, with particularly strong improvements when gaze targets are not semantically salient. Our findings offer valuable insights for advancing future gaze following research. We will release the code once the paper is accepted.

中文摘要翻译: 注视跟随既需要场景理解，也需要注视推理来定位场景中人物的注视目标。近年来，视觉基础模型（VFM）在该任务上表现出强大的性能，能够以更简单的架构超越先前方法。然而，我们观察到基于VFM的方法存在一个关键局限：虽然VFM大幅提升了场景理解能力，但它们对注视推理几乎没有贡献。因此，现有方法往往依赖语义显著物体而非真实的注视线索，导致当目标不显著时性能下降。为解决这一问题，我们提出了一种新的训练机制来增强VFM在注视跟随任务中的注视推理能力。我们的方法包括：(1) 头条件局部LoRA，通过局部适配在保留场景token学习的同时改进头部token的注视推理能力；(2) 锥外惩罚，将注视线索注入头部token，同时使其与场景token对齐。在GazeFollow和VAT数据集上的实验表明，我们的方法达到了最先进的性能，尤其当注视目标不具语义显著性时提升显著。我们的发现为推进未来注视跟随研究提供了有价值的见解。论文接收后将发布代码。

</details>


### [86] [GLeVE: Graph-Guided Lesion Grounding with Proposal Verification in 3D CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22619)
*Shuo Jiang,Yuhao Hong,Chunbo Jiang,Weihong Chen,Huangwei Chen,Shenghao Zhu,Beining Wu,Mingxuan Liu,Zhu Zhu,Feiwei Qin,Min Tan,Yifei Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GLeVE框架，通过图引导的病变定位、解剖先验验证和八叉树自回归细化，实现CT报告中病变描述到3D体积的精确对齐与分割。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法依赖短语级对齐或密集像素监督，导致病变级对应关系不足和定位精度不佳，难以弥合自由文本与三维解剖结构之间的语义-空间鸿沟。

Method: 将每个病变描述视为原子语义单元，通过关系感知图推理编码器官归属、属性及病变间关系生成判别性病变查询；采用解剖感知的区域提议生成与区域级验证实现一对一文本-病变对齐；利用层次化八叉树细化逐步改善边界描绘。

Result: 在AbdomenAtlas 3.0数据集上，该方法在分割精度和病变级定位方面均优于经典多模态基础模型和报告监督基线方法。

Conclusion: GLeVE框架通过图引导的语义-空间对齐与渐进式细化，有效提升了放射学报告到3D CT的病变级定位与分割性能，为可验证的临床解释提供了可行方案。

Original Abstract: Grounding radiology report descriptions to 3D CT volumes is essential for verifiable clinical interpretation, yet remains challenging due to the semantic-spatial gap between free-text narratives and volumetric anatomy. Existing report-assisted and vision-language grounding methods typically rely on phrase-level alignment or dense pixel supervision, resulting in limited lesion-wise correspondence and suboptimal localization accuracy. We propose GLeVE, a graph-guided lesion grounding framework with anatomical prior verification and octree-based autoregressive refinement. GLeVE treats each lesion description as an atomic semantic unit and encodes organ attribution, attributes, and inter-lesion relations through relation-aware graph reasoning to produce discriminative lesion-wise queries. Anatomy-aware proposal generation with region-level verification enforces one-to-one text-lesion alignment, while hierarchical octree refinement progressively improves boundary delineation. Experiments on AbdomenAtlas 3.0 demonstrate consistent gains over classical multimodal foundation models and report-supervised baselines in both segmentation accuracy and lesion-level localization.

中文摘要翻译: 将放射学报告描述定位到3D CT体积对于可验证的临床解释至关重要，但由于自由文本叙述与体积解剖结构之间的语义-空间鸿沟，这一任务仍然具有挑战性。现有的报告辅助和视觉-语言定位方法通常依赖于短语级对齐或密集像素监督，导致病变级对应关系不足和定位精度不佳。我们提出GLeVE，一个具有解剖先验验证和八叉树自回归细化的图引导病变定位框架。GLeVE将每个病变描述视为一个原子语义单元，通过关系感知图推理编码器官归属、属性及病变间关系，以生成判别性的病变级查询。具有区域级验证的解剖感知提议生成实现了一对一的文本-病变对齐，而层次化八叉树细化则逐步改善了边界描绘。在AbdomenAtlas 3.0上的实验表明，该方法在分割精度和病变级定位方面均优于经典多模态基础模型和报告监督基线方法。

</details>


### [87] [AtomicMotion: Learning Human Motion From Different Human Parts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22631)
*Runzhen Liu,Chuhua Xian,Fa-Ting Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AtomicMotion通过解耦与重新整合身体动力学，提出逻辑身体划分、掩码全身预条件化策略和运动学注意力机制，从稀疏头手轨迹中高保真重建全身姿态，在AMASS数据集上显著优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法将人体视为单一整体，无法捕捉细粒度的"原子意图"，且忽略了人体固有的结构拓扑，导致误差累积和关节协调不自然。

Method: 1）将骨骼分解为五个功能意图驱动的逻辑簇，保持内部关节协同；2）采用掩码全身预条件化训练策略，使模型内化全局骨骼拓扑与潜在运动学约束；3）提出运动学注意力机制，将经典运动学树结构嵌入注意力，确保生物合理性。

Result: 在AMASS数据集上，AtomicMotion显著优于现有基线方法，实现了更高的重建保真度和更优的生物力学真实感。

Conclusion: AtomicMotion通过解耦与再整合身体动力学，有效解决了从稀疏轨迹重建全身姿态中的误差积累和关节协调问题，为沉浸式AR/VR远程呈现提供了有力支持。

Original Abstract: Accurately reconstructing full-body poses from sparse head and hand trajectories is a foundational challenge for immersive AR/VR telepresence. Current methods often struggle with error accumulation and unnatural joint coordination, primarily because they treat the human body as a monolithic entity, thereby failing to capture the fine-grained ``atomic intents'' embedded in subtle signal variations and overlooking the inherent structural topology. To bridge this gap, we present AtomicMotion, a framework designed to decouple and re-integrate body dynamics through three core innovations. First, we introduce a logical body partitioning scheme that decomposes the skeleton into five distinct clusters based on functional intent; this ensures that each partition preserves internal joint synergies while isolating local motion primitives. Second, to robustly map sparse inputs to high-dimensional poses, we employ a masked full-body pre-conditioning strategy during training, forcing the model to internalize global skeletal topology and latent kinematic constraints. Finally, addressing the limitations of vanilla spatial attention, which often ignores fixed physiological connectivity, we propose Kinematic Attention. By embedding the classical kinematic tree structure into the attention mechanism, we ensure biological plausibility in the synthesized motions. Extensive evaluations on the AMASS dataset demonstrate that AtomicMotion significantly outperforms existing baselines, yielding higher reconstruction fidelity and superior biomechanical realism.

中文摘要翻译: 从稀疏的头部和手部轨迹中精确重建全身姿态，是沉浸式AR/VR远程呈现的一项基础性挑战。现有方法通常受困于误差累积和关节协调不自然，主要原因在于它们将人体视为一个整体，从而无法捕捉嵌入在细微信号变化中的细粒度"原子意图"，并忽略了固有的结构拓扑。为弥合这一差距，我们提出了AtomicMotion，一个通过三项核心创新来解耦并重新整合身体动力学的框架。首先，我们引入了一种逻辑身体划分方案，基于功能意图将骨骼分解为五个不同的聚类；这确保了每个分区在隔离局部运动基元的同时，保持内部关节协同。其次，为将稀疏输入鲁棒地映射到高维姿态，我们在训练过程中采用了一种掩码全身预条件化策略，迫使模型内化全局骨骼拓扑和潜在运动学约束。最后，针对通常忽略固定生理连接性的普通空间注意力的局限性，我们提出了运动学注意力。通过将经典运动学树结构嵌入注意力机制，我们确保了合成运动中的生物合理性。在AMASS数据集上的广泛评估表明，AtomicMotion显著优于现有基线，实现了更高的重建保真度和更优的生物力学真实感。

</details>


### [88] [From Baseline to Follow-Up: Counterfactual Spine DXA Image Synthesis in UK Biobank Using a Causal Hierarchical Variational Autoencoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22649)
*Yilin Zhang,Nicholas C. Harvey,Nicholas R. Fuggle,Rahman Attar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种元数据条件因果层次变分自编码器（CHVAE），用于生成因果一致的AP脊柱DXA图像，并在UK Biobank数据上验证了年龄干预下的反事实形态测量一致性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 尽管DXA广泛用于骨骼评估，但学习可控、可解释且具有特定因子解剖变异的生成模型仍具挑战性，尤其是在保证因果一致性方面。

Method: 提出元数据条件因果层次变分自编码器（CHVAE），以参与者基本属性和腰椎形态测量为条件，在UKB的3,743张AP脊柱DXA图像上训练，并通过诱因-动作-预测（AAP）框架评估因果一致性：从基线图像诱因隐变量，对年龄进行干预至重复成像时的值，生成反事实随访形态测量并与真实观测对比。

Result: 在年龄干预下，关键椎骨形态测量变量展现出强绝对水平一致性，支持干预对齐的、解剖学合理的DXA图像合成。

Conclusion: 所提出的CHVAE模型能够生成因果一致且解剖学合理的DXA图像，为可控、可解释的医学图像生成提供了有效方法。

Original Abstract: Dual-energy X-ray absorptiometry (DXA) is widely used for large-scale skeletal assessment, yet learning controllable and interpretable factor-specific anatomical variation remains challenging. We propose a metadata-conditioned causal hierarchical variational autoencoder (CHVAE) for causally consistent generation of anteroposterior (AP) spine DXA images from the UK Biobank (UKB). The model is trained on 3,743 raw AP spine scans from the first imaging visit and conditioned on basic participant attributes and lumbar morphometry. Causal consistency is evaluated in a baseline-to-follow-up setting using abduction--action--prediction (AAP): latent variables are abducted from baseline images, age is intervened to the repeat-imaging value, and the resulting counterfactual follow-up morphometry is compared with observed repeat-imaging measurements. Results show strong absolute-level agreement for key vertebral morphometry variables under age intervention, supporting intervention-aligned synthesis of anatomically plausible DXA images.

中文摘要翻译: 双能X射线吸收测定法（DXA）广泛用于大规模骨骼评估，但学习可控且可解释的因子特异性解剖变异仍具有挑战性。我们提出了一种元数据条件因果层次变分自编码器（CHVAE），用于从英国生物银行（UKB）生成因果一致的前后位（AP）脊柱DXA图像。该模型在首次影像访问的3,743张原始AP脊柱扫描上训练，并以参与者基本属性和腰椎形态测量为条件。因果一致性在基线到随访的设置中通过诱因-动作-预测（AAP）进行评估：从基线图像诱因出隐变量，将年龄干预为重复成像时的值，并将得到的反事实随访形态测量与观测到的重复成像测量进行比较。结果表明，在年龄干预下，关键椎骨形态测量变量表现出强绝对水平一致性，支持干预对齐的、解剖学合理的DXA图像合成。

</details>


### [89] [SEGA: Spectral-Energy Guided Attention for Resolution Extrapolation in Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22668)
*Javad Rajabi,Kimia Shaban,Koorosh Roohi,David B. Lindell,Babak Taati*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SEGA方法，通过根据潜变量空间频率结构动态缩放RoPE组件注意力，实现无需训练的高分辨率文本到图像生成，在结构连贯性和细节保真度上超越现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有DiT模型在超出训练分辨率的图像生成中性能下降，而现有的免训练方法对RoPE组件采用统一且内容无关的缩放策略，导致在保持全局结构与恢复细粒度细节之间存在权衡。

Method: SEGA是一种免训练方法，在每次去噪步骤中根据潜变量的空间-频率结构动态调整各RoPE组件的注意力缩放，实现自适应缩放以兼顾结构连贯性和细节保真度。

Result: SEGA在多个目标分辨率下持续改进高分辨率合成质量，超越了当前最先进的免训练基线方法。

Conclusion: SEGA通过动态自适应缩放有效解决了统一缩放带来的结构-细节权衡问题，在不需额外训练的条件下显著提升了DiT模型的高分辨率生成能力。

Original Abstract: Diffusion transformers (DiTs) have emerged as a dominant architecture for text-to-image generation, yet their performance drops when generating at resolutions beyond their training range. Existing training-free approaches mitigate this by modifying inference-time attention behavior, often through Rotary Position Embeddings (RoPE) extrapolation combined with attention scaling. However, these strategies apply a uniform and content-agnostic scaling across RoPE components with distinct frequency characteristics, inducing a trade-off between preserving global structure and recovering fine detail. We introduce SEGA, a training-free method that dynamically scales attention across RoPE components according to the latent's spatial-frequency structure at each denoising step. This adaptive scaling improves both structural coherence and fine-detail fidelity. Experiments show that SEGA consistently improves high-resolution synthesis across multiple target resolutions, outperforming state-of-the-art training-free baselines.

中文摘要翻译: 扩散变换器（DiTs）已成为文本到图像生成的主导架构，但其在超出训练范围的分辨率下生成时性能下降。现有的免训练方法通过修改推理时的注意力行为来缓解这一问题，通常采用旋转位置编码（RoPE）外推结合注意力缩放。然而，这些策略对具有不同频率特性的RoPE组件施加统一且内容无关的缩放，导致在保留全局结构与恢复细粒度细节之间存在权衡。我们提出SEGA，一种免训练方法，它根据每次去噪步骤中潜变量的空间-频率结构动态缩放RoPE组件的注意力。这种自适应缩放同时改善了结构连贯性和细粒度细节保真度。实验表明，SEGA在多个目标分辨率下持续提升高分辨率合成质量，优于当前最先进的免训练基线方法。

</details>


### [90] [From Abstraction to Instantiation: Learning Behavioral Representation for Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22671)
*Bing Hu,Zaijing Li,Rui Shao,Junda Chen,April Hua Liu,Wei-Shi Zheng,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BehaviorVLA通过因果Mamba架构的视动行为编码器和阶段条件行为解码器，学习时间一致的行为表示，在分布偏移下实现鲁棒的机器人操作，在多个基准上达到SOTA，且仅需50%演示数据即可匹配OpenVLA-OFT的sim-to-real迁移性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有VLA模型在分布偏移下性能下降，因为难以学习跨不同环境的泛化行为表示；已有的基于动作的潜变量方法受限于短时域碎片化和静态执行对齐，导致复杂场景中行为不一致。

Method: 提出BehaviorVLA框架，包含两个对称组件：(1) 视动行为编码器(VBE)，采用因果Mamba架构聚合长时域轨迹信息为统一行为表示；(2) 阶段条件行为解码器(PBD)，通过动态对齐任务级先验与实时执行进度，将行为表示解码为精确动作。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0(58%)、LIBERO(98%)和CALVIN(平均长度4.36)上取得SOTA成功率；在真实sim-to-real迁移中，仅用50%演示数据即匹配OpenVLA-OFT的性能，展现出优越的数据效率和泛化能力。

Conclusion: BehaviorVLA通过时间一致的行为表示学习，有效解决了分布偏移下的鲁棒操作问题，在多个基准和数据效率方面均优于现有方法。

Original Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models often suffer from performance degradation under distribution shifts, as they struggle to learn generalized behavior representations across varying environments.
